A practical reading time is about 30–45 minutes without following every proof.2f:T7b8,The method has three main components. First, the authors fit an activation manifold and a behavior manifold. They use tasks with known latent structure: cyclic concepts such as weekdays and months, sequential concepts such as ages and letters, graph-structured concepts learned in context, and physical position in a video world model. For each task, they collect internal activations from a chosen layer and fit a representation manifold Mh. In parallel, they restrict the output distribution to task-relevant concepts and fit a behavior manifold My. The important point is not just visualization, but the use of manifold geometry and geodesic distances.

Second, they introduce geometry-aware steering. The baseline is standard linear steering, which adds a vector in activation space and scales its magnitude. Their alternative, manifold steering, moves activations along geodesic or approximately geodesic paths on Mh rather than along straight Euclidean lines. The motivation is to keep interventions near the support of natural activations.

Third, they test the link between representation geometry and behavior geometry. One result is a scaled isometry relation: geodesic distances on Mh align closely with geodesic distances on My, while Euclidean distances do not. The other result is path-level causality: steering along Mh induces output trajectories that stay close to My. They also run the reverse experiment by directly optimizing for paths that remain natural in behavior space; the recovered activation trajectories bend to follow Mh rather than staying linear. That reverse recovery makes the argument stronger than a simple “our steering works better” claim.

The main conceptual contribution is to reframe steering from a problem of choosing a direction to a problem of choosing a geometry. In that language, linear steering is just geodesic motion under a flat metric, while manifold steering uses a metric induced by natural activations or behaviors.30:T5ae,The experiments span both language models and a video world model. On the language side, the tasks include cyclic concepts such as weekdays and months, ordered concepts such as ages and letters, and graph-structured concepts learned in context. The main empirical findings are:

1. Distance alignment: geodesic distances on the activation manifold and the behavior manifold are close to isometric up to a scale factor, whereas Euclidean distances fail to capture the same structure.
2. Naturalness of trajectories: linear steering often passes through low-density regions of behavior space and produces unnatural intermediate distributions, degraded fluency, or diversity collapse; manifold steering yields trajectories that more closely match the model’s natural output transitions.
3. Reverse recovery: when the optimization is performed in behavior space to stay on My, the resulting activation trajectory bends to follow the curvature of Mh rather than remaining a straight line.
4. Cross-modal consistency: similar geometry-behavior coupling appears in the video world model for physical position, suggesting the effect is not limited to toy text tasks.

The excerpt provided does not include the full quantitative tables, so it is better to focus on the structure of the evidence than to invent exact numbers. The paper is driven by geometric alignment, trajectory visualizations, and intervention quality rather than by a single benchmark score.31:T437,This is a skim paper unless you work on interpretability or activation control. Start with the introduction and the main setup. The key idea to retain is simple: the paper is not searching for a better steering vector, but for the right geometry of activation space. Then look at the weekday or month examples first, because cyclic concepts make the difference between straight-line and manifold-following interventions visually obvious. After that, read the sections showing geodesic-distance alignment between Mh and My; that is the central empirical support for the paper’s claim.

If you only spend 20–30 minutes, focus on three parts: task setup, the manifold-steering procedure, and the comparison figures between linear and manifold interventions. If you want the stronger causal argument, read the reverse optimization experiment where behavior-space constraints recover curved activation trajectories. For pretraining researchers, the useful takeaway is not immediate adoption, but a caution: many representation analyses that look linear may only be locally linear.32:T462,The paper constructs shallow-but-wide transformers where the approximation power is placed in the attention blocks themselves, not in separate deep FFNs. Through carefully designed ReLU-activated attention interactions, the model computes interpretable nonlinear features of inputs (polynomial features or B-spline bases). A final linear-attention layer then approximates the least-squares solution over these features, yielding an end-to-end in-context nonlinear regressor.
Compared to modular approaches that assume universal approximation via deep feedforward networks and then apply linear attention, this work emphasizes fully constructive feature realization (claimed to be exact for the chosen bases) and depth that does not scale with target accuracy; it highlights, for instance, a two-layer attention-only construction for regression with a linear cardinal B-spline basis.
The theory derives finite-sample error bounds with an explicit bias–variance decomposition: approximation error from polynomial/spline approximation of the function class, and statistical error from finite context length and sample size.33:T7db,The method has two layers.

First, the paper analyzes why 50% is the right target. For binary rewards, reward entropy is maximized at p=0.5. Under GRPO/RLOO, the magnitude of relative advantage signal is also strongest when successes and failures are balanced. The number of success-failure contrastive pairs peaks near k=N/2 in a group of size N, and group filtering is most likely to retain groups in that middle regime. This gives a principled explanation for why groups like 1/8 or 7/8, while not fully discarded, are still low-value training samples.

Second, the paper introduces Prefix Sampling (PS) to steer skewed groups toward that regime. With N=8 rollouts per group, groups with k in {0,8} are discarded, groups with k in {3,4,5} are used as-is, groups with k in {1,2} are treated as too hard, and groups with k in {6,7} as too easy. For too-hard groups, PS replays a successful prefix as a head start for new continuations. For too-easy groups, it replays a failing prefix as a handicap. The goal is not simply to make trajectories easier or harder, but to move the continuation pass rate toward the high-information middle.

A key implementation detail is that the method works in stateful multi-turn environments by reconstructing the environment state through the replayed prefix, while masking replayed prefix tokens from the loss. Only the fresh continuation generated by the current policy is optimized. This matters because otherwise the method would partly collapse into behavior cloning on old trajectories. The paper also uses an adaptive controller that chooses a prefix ratio r_b in [0.05, 0.95] for each pass-rate bucket, determining how much of a trajectory to replay.

Relative to prior hinting or filtering methods, the differences are clear: no external teacher or oracle solution is required, the control target is group pass-rate distribution rather than single-trajectory success, and the method is designed for long, stateful agent rollouts rather than static problem settings.34:T50d,The main empirical claim is improved training efficiency at matched final performance.

1. SWE-bench-style agentic RL: on Qwen3-14B and Qwen3-32B, Prefix Sampling reaches the baseline’s best verified score in up to 1.76x fewer benchmark-evaluation steps. The reported end-to-end speedups are 2.01x for Qwen3-14B and 1.55x for Qwen3-32B, with no loss in final performance.

2. Transfer beyond software engineering: the paper also mentions gains on AIME 2025 reasoning, suggesting the method is not tied to one environment family. Still, the core setting remains binary-reward, group-based RLVR.

3. Mechanistic evidence: the authors claim that the gains come from steering rerollout pass rates toward 50%, not from generic replay or data augmentation. This is an important point. If the plots indeed show pass-rate redistribution as the mediator, the paper’s argument is much stronger.

4. Ablations to check closely: whether both directions matter (head starts for hard groups and handicaps for easy groups), whether adaptive prefix length beats fixed replay length, whether masking replayed tokens is necessary, and how sensitive the method is to bucket boundaries. These ablations determine whether the contribution is really about pass-rate control or just a favorable replay heuristic.35:T42f,This is not a core pretraining paper, but it is relevant at the pretrain/post-train interface, especially for researchers interested in tool use, agentic RL, multimodal data construction, and training recipes. Its main value comes from three places. First, it turns search capability into a data-generation problem with explicit structure, rather than relying only on manual annotation or closed trajectory distillation. That is useful for future knowledge-intensive multimodal training sets. Second, the fatal-aware RL design addresses a real failure mode in long-horizon agent training: useful reasoning often exists before a rollout collapses, and standard filtering wastes it. Third, the paper integrates visual preprocessing tools with retrieval tools in one decision loop, which is an important reminder that multimodal agent performance depends not only on reasoning but also on making evidence legible. The contribution is substantial within the agent setting, though it is better viewed as a post-training/agent recipe than as a new foundation-modeling paradigm.36:T5de,The approach has three components: data, tools, and training. For data, the authors start from the Wikipedia hyperlink graph, sample multi-hop entity paths, and convert them into image-grounded question-answer instances by rewriting intermediate entities into fuzzy descriptions. This is meant to suppress shortcut behavior such as one-hop image lookup or direct string matching. They further add source-anchor visual grounding so that the question, visible image anchors, source pages, and downstream retrieval chain remain executable as a training trajectory. The resulting datasets are SearchVL-SFT-36k for supervised fine-tuning and SearchVL-RL-8k for agentic RL. For tools, the environment includes not only search but also OCR, cropping, sharpening, super-resolution, and perspective correction. That design choice is practical: many real images are blurry, skewed, low-resolution, or cluttered, so retrieval quality depends on visual cleanup before querying external knowledge. For training, the paper builds on GRPO for long-horizon multimodal tool use and introduces two modifications for fatal failures. Fatal-aware token masking removes invalid post-failure suffix tokens from optimization once a tool call irrecoverably fails. One-sided advantage clamping preserves useful pre-failure reasoning and action segments so partially successful rollouts still contribute learning signal. Together, these changes target a common agent-RL problem: coarse credit assignment under cascading failures.37:T538,The evaluation covers seven multimodal deep-search benchmarks. The headline result is an average score improvement from 47.8 to 61.6 over a Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B agentic baseline, a gain of 13.8 points. Reported benchmark gains include +13.3 on VDR, +24.5 on MMSearch, +10.2 on FVQA, and +16.2 on InfoSeek. The main model appears to be OpenSearch-VL-30B-A3B built on top of Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B. The paper also claims performance comparable to, and in some cases better than, proprietary commercial systems on several benchmarks; when reading closely, it is worth checking tool budgets and evaluation conditions for those comparisons. The most informative parts are likely the ablations: how much comes from multi-hop path sampling, fuzzy entity rewriting, and filtering in the data pipeline; how much the visual preprocessing tools help on degraded images; and how much each RL component contributes. The paper explicitly argues that discarding failed trajectories wastes useful pre-failure reasoning, while keeping full failed rollouts injects noisy gradients, so the RL modifications are intended to produce stable gains specifically in long-horizon settings. If the full tables are available, the most useful metrics to inspect are success rate, average number of tool steps, failure-type breakdown, and gains by difficulty or image quality.38:T45b,This one deserves a full read, but with a clear agenda. Start with the introduction and method overview to frame the three bottlenecks: data, tools, and RL. Then read the data-construction section carefully, especially Wikipedia path sampling, fuzzy entity rewriting, source-anchor grounding, and filtering rules. That part determines whether the model is learning genuine multi-hop search or just templated retrieval. Next, read the RL section in detail, focusing on the formal definition of fatal failure, the masking boundary, the one-sided advantage clamping rule, and the rollout examples. The key questions are why this is preferable to trajectory-level filtering, what bias the clamping may introduce, and how much useful signal survives after masking. In experiments, prioritize the main benchmark table, data ablations, tool ablations, RL ablations, and any breakdown by image quality or question type. About one hour is enough to get the main ideas; if you work on agent RL or multimodal post-training, two hours on the appendix is justified for prompts, tool interfaces, reward design, and failure cases.39:T8e4,方法核心是 TSCG，一个确定性的 tool-schema compiler。输入是标准 JSON schema，输出是 token-efficient 的结构化文本表示；不需要访问模型参数，不做微调，也不做运行时搜索。作者把整个系统拆成 8 个可组合算子，并按固定顺序组成 10 步 pipeline。设计思路很明确：不是学习一个 schema rewriter，而是把若干已知的 transformer 读取偏好编码成规则。

几类关键算子分别对应不同机制。第一类是纯压缩型变换，如 delimiter 选择、删除 filler token、把冗长结构短语替换成紧凑分隔符。这部分利用的是 BPE 非单调性和 tokenizer 差异，例如某些符号在特定 tokenizer 下比自然语言短得多。第二类是重排型变换，如把输出约束前置到 prompt 开头，利用 attention sink；把多步操作按拓扑顺序排列，保证因果可达性。第三类是增强型变换，如在结尾追加 closure block，或按 fragility 分数复制关键语义原子，利用 recency bias 和首尾位置优势。

论文里一个值得注意的点是，它没有把所有算子都当成“越多越好”。作者明确区分了 token-reducing、structure-preserving、token-expanding 三类操作，并给出形式化定义与语义保持条件 sem(τ(p)) ⊇ sem(p)。更重要的是，他们证明了若只看一组压缩算子，well-formed schema 上可保证至少 51% 的 token 压缩。这个理论结果不复杂，但足够说明该系统不是经验拼装。

方法上的另一个亮点是 format-versus-compression 分解。作者比较 JSON baseline 与文本 baseline，发现准确率与 token 数的相关性在 JSON 表示下很强，R²=0.88；换成文本表示后几乎消失，R²=0.03。这个结果支持他们的主张：决定性因素是 representation change，而不是简单缩短 prompt。也就是说，模型不是单纯“上下文太长看不过来”，而是“看不懂这种结构”。

从预训练研究视角看，TSCG 的真正价值不在规则本身，而在它把 schema 理解问题拆成了 tokenizer 友好性、位置布局、因果可访问性、关键原子冗余保护这几个可分析维度。这些维度都可以反过来指导数据合成和预训练格式设计。3a:T837,TSCG is a deterministic compiler that converts JSON tool schemas into a compact structured-text format. It is model-agnostic, requires no finetuning, and does not rely on runtime search. The system is built from eight composable operators arranged in a fixed pipeline. The design philosophy is to encode known transformer reading biases into deterministic rewrites rather than learn a schema rewriter.

The operators fall into three groups. Token-reducing operators compress the schema by choosing tokenizer-friendly delimiters, removing filler phrases, and replacing verbose structural language with compact symbolic forms. These exploit BPE non-monotonicity and tokenizer-specific token counts. Structure-preserving reordering operators move critical constraints to the beginning of the prompt to exploit attention sink effects, and reorder multi-step operations topologically so prerequisites are causally accessible. Token-expanding operators add redundancy near the end of the prompt or duplicate fragile semantic atoms to exploit recency bias and boundary-position advantages.

A strong point is that the paper does not treat all operators as uniformly beneficial. It formalizes semantic preservation, distinguishes reducing vs. reordering vs. expanding transforms, and proves a >=51% compression guarantee on well-formed schemas for the reducing subset. More importantly, the paper isolates representation change from raw compression. On JSON baselines, accuracy is strongly correlated with token count (R²=0.88), but on text baselines that correlation nearly disappears (R²=0.03). That result supports the central claim: the dominant effect is not just shorter context, but a representation that the model can actually parse.

From a pretraining perspective, the deeper value is the decomposition itself. The paper turns schema understanding into analyzable dimensions: tokenizer friendliness, positional layout, causal accessibility, and redundancy allocation for fragile semantic atoms. Those dimensions could inform data formatting and serialization choices in future pretraining pipelines.3b:T6df,The empirical section is broad enough to be credible. The authors build TSCG-Agentic-Bench with roughly 19,000 API calls, 12 models, and 5 scenarios, covering 4B–32B models plus three frontier systems. The headline result is that several small models recover from near-collapse under raw JSON schemas to usable tool-calling accuracy under TSCG. The most visible example is Phi-4 14B: from 0% to 84.4% accuracy at 20 tools, and 90.3% at 50 tools.

On efficiency, the compiler preserves 52%–57% token savings even at 100 tools, so the compression benefit does not vanish at larger catalog sizes. On heavier real-world MCP schemas, the method still yields about +5.0 percentage points, suggesting the gains are not limited to lightweight synthetic catalogs. The paper also reports external validation on BFCL with 108%–181% ARR improvements.

The ablations are the most informative part. Some operators are clearly scale-sensitive. CFL and CFO help at smaller catalog sizes but become harmful beyond around 43 tools, which suggests positional anchoring and reordering can start competing for limited attention budget in long contexts. CCP, despite being theoretically motivated, shows no measurable gain in an 11-model ablation while adding about 85–306 tokens; the authors explicitly acknowledge this. That makes the paper more trustworthy. Another useful finding is the model-specific operator-response taxonomy: operator-hungry, operator-sensitive, and operator-robust. There is no universal best configuration.

A final result worth noting is that the synthetic TAB benchmark predicts real MCP performance within 0.1 accuracy points. If robust, that means the benchmark is capturing the main failure mechanism rather than a narrow synthetic artifact.3c:T444,For a skim, start with the introduction, Figure 1, the operator definitions in Section 3, and then jump to the main result tables plus the operator ablations such as Table 23. Around 25–35 minutes should be enough to extract the main ideas.

Focus on three questions while reading. First, how the paper turns the intuition that “JSON is bad for LLMs” into a testable claim, especially through the format-vs-compression decomposition. Second, which operators are consistently useful and which are only theoretically motivated. Third, why some operators help at small tool counts but hurt at larger ones; that scale sensitivity is the part most relevant to transformer behavior.

If you want to go deeper, Appendix E on fragility scoring and budget allocation is worth a look. It is presented as an engineering heuristic, but it maps onto a broader research question: under a fixed context budget, how should structured semantic atoms be reordered and redundantly encoded? That question extends naturally to pretraining data formats, RAG schema design, and multimodal token serialization.3d:T642,Uno-Orchestra is a single causal-LM policy that emits both a task decomposition and a routing decision for every subtask. The routing unit is not just a model ID. It is a (model, primitive) pair, where a primitive is an atomic routable action: an internal reasoning mode, a multi-step skill invocation, or an external tool call. This collapses planning and dispatch into one shared backbone, instead of running a planner first and a dispatcher later.

Training has two stages. Stage one is supervised fine-tuning on a verifier-gated curriculum of 61,201 teacher-distilled trajectories from 38 public datasets, disjoint from evaluation. A notable detail is that code and tool subtasks are executed in real sandboxes, so observations are runtime outputs rather than synthetic traces. Stage two applies Agentic-GRPO on the residual hard pool. This extends GRPO to multi-turn orchestration by using structural cues from the generated plan to assign observation-level and process-level intermediate rewards, which helps with long-horizon credit assignment.

Several design choices are worth noting. First, decomposition and routing are merged because separate modules create context-passing overhead and error propagation, while a unified policy allows budget constraints to act directly on the full decision space. Second, the worker pool and primitive vocabulary are closed, which makes RL tractable and cost-accountable. Third, admissible actions are sparse: not every model supports every primitive. That turns the problem into constrained combinatorial control rather than generic free-form generation.3e:T56f,The evaluation spans 13 benchmarks across math, code, knowledge, long-context, and agentic tool use, with comparisons against 22 baselines. The headline result is 77.0% macro pass@1, about 16% above the strongest workflow baseline, at roughly an order-of-magnitude lower per-query cost. That matters because it suggests the gain is not simply from using more calls, but from finer-grained selective delegation.

The training set is relatively modest: 61,201 SFT trajectories plus RL on a hard subset. That makes the improvement more attributable to action-space design and training signals than to brute-force imitation scale. The most informative comparisons are likely the following: against flat routers, which cannot assign different subtasks to different capability-cost tiers; against hand-crafted hierarchical workflows, which decompose but do not jointly optimize dispatch; and ablations removing the unified policy, verifier-gated curriculum, or Agentic-GRPO.

The abstract does not expose all implementation details such as controller size, worker pool composition, or per-benchmark breakdowns. When reading the full paper, the details that matter most are the primitive taxonomy, how cost is calibrated, how the correctness–cost trade-off coefficient is set, and how the hard pool is constructed. Those choices determine whether the method is portable beyond the reported setup.3f:T430,Read it as a policy-learning paper, not as another generic multi-agent workflow paper. Three parts deserve attention.

First, inspect the action representation. The key question is how a single assistant turn encodes subtasks, dependencies, and one (model, primitive) choice per subtask. If that schema is weak, the whole method reduces to prompt engineering; if it is clean, the paper is really about structured control through a causal LM.

Second, focus on the training pipeline, especially verifier-gated curriculum construction and Agentic-GRPO. Those sections contain the practical answer to how they keep trajectory quality high and how they make multi-turn RL learnable.

Third, read the main results and ablations with a systems lens: where does selective delegation help most, does the gain come from deeper decomposition or cheaper worker assignment, and how stable is the quality–cost trade-off.

A 25–35 minute skim should be enough for the main ideas. For deeper reading, check the appendix for the action schema, primitive vocabulary, and failure cases.40:T66f,The main paradigm is Context-ReAct, which augments the standard ReAct loop with explicit context meta-actions. At each step, the agent jointly generates its reasoning trace, a set of context-edit operations, and the next tool call in one autoregressive pass. The meta-operations are applied before the next observation is appended, so the agent actively curates its working memory online.

The operation vocabulary has five atomic actions. Skip leaves the context unchanged. Compress replaces a contiguous span of prior steps with an abstractive summary. Snippet preserves an exact substring from a previous step, which is useful for numbers, entity names, quotations, or code where abstraction is risky. Delete removes a step that no longer carries value. Rollback abandons an unproductive branch by reverting to an earlier state while retaining the reason for backtracking and any transferable insight.

The key idea is not merely adding more actions. It is changing the representation of context from a uniform text log into a multi-resolution working memory. The paper also argues expressive completeness: in principle, Compress can simulate the other operations, but specialized operators reduce generation cost and fidelity loss. That is a sensible engineering choice, similar to designing a practical instruction set rather than relying on a theoretically minimal one.

The instantiated system, LongSeeker, is fine-tuned from Qwen3-30B-A3B on 10k synthesized search trajectories. The important training detail is that the data must teach not only how to search, but when to preserve exact evidence, when to summarize, and when to roll back.41:T53d,LongSeeker is evaluated on BrowseComp, BrowseComp-ZH, xbench, and GAIA. The headline numbers in the paper are 61.5% on BrowseComp and 62.5% on BrowseComp-ZH, compared with 43.2% and 46.7% for Tongyi DeepResearch, and 36.2% and 47.3% for AgentFold. These gains suggest that elastic context control matters most in long-horizon, tool-augmented search rather than in short QA settings.

The most informative experiments are likely threefold. First, comparisons against sliding-window, periodic summarization, and restart-style baselines, to test whether adaptive context editing really beats fixed policies. Second, ablations over the five operations, especially Snippet and Rollback. Those two are the clearest indicators that the method is doing more than generic summarization. Third, analyses of context length, token cost, and success rate. If the paper reports trajectory-level statistics, the important question is whether the agent maintains or improves accuracy while operating on a shorter and cleaner context.

The training scale is modest at 10k synthesized trajectories, so the result is more about the usefulness of the paradigm than about data scale. One caveat to watch is whether the synthesis pipeline bakes in a particular search style from the teacher, which could limit generalization if the evaluation distribution shifts.42:T448,This is best read as a context-interface paper rather than a benchmark paper. Start with the method section and understand exactly what Context-ReAct adds over standard ReAct. The most important part is the semantics of the five operations and the failure modes they are meant to address. Snippet and Rollback deserve special attention because they are the strongest departures from ordinary summarization.

Then read the data synthesis and training pipeline. The key question is where supervision for the meta-operations comes from and how the trajectories are constructed. If that part is weak, the whole method risks collapsing into a prompt-level heuristic.

Finally, inspect the main results and ablations with an efficiency lens: whether gains come with lower token cost, which operations are used most often, and whether failures are caused by bad compression, bad deletion, or poor search policy.

A 20–30 minute skim is enough for the main ideas. If reading selectively, prioritize the paradigm figure, the operation ablation table, and any statistics on context length or token usage.43:T550,LCM uses a dual-state memory design:
- Immutable Store: append-only, verbatim persistence of every user/assistant/tool message; the single source of truth.
- Active Context: the prompt actually sent to the LLM each turn, composed of recent raw messages plus Summary Nodes for older history.
Older content is compacted into Summary Nodes produced by LLM summarization, but “lossless” means every summary retains pointers to the underlying raw messages, which remain reachable via tools like lcm_grep and lcm_expand. The memory is organized as a high-fanout DAG stored in a transactional, indexed persistent database (PostgreSQL in the reference implementation), enabling multi-resolution traversal of session history.
Key design choice vs RLM: RLM grants the model full autonomy to write recursive scripts (high expressivity, high variance, brittle in production, plus overhead on short queries). LCM constrains memory operations into a small set of deterministic, engine-executed primitives—analogous to structured programming replacing GOTO—trading theoretical expressivity for reliability and reproducibility. It also argues why flat-file grep is insufficient for open-ended recall and why embedding-RAG can lose conversational structure; the DAG summaries aim to preserve decision provenance while still However, gradients in different network blocks exhibit distinct behaviors, such as varying directional stability and scale anisotropy, implying that expensive optimizer states are not universally necessary and using a global optimizer is often memory-inefficient. We propose the Budget-Aware Optimizer Configurator (BAOC) to reduce memory cost by assigning suitable optimizer configurations to individual blocks under given bu","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-06T10:02:31+00:00","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2605_04711.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG","math.OC"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是大模型训练中优化器状态显存过高的问题。核心观察是：不同网络块的梯度行为并不相同，有的块方向稳定、适合动量累积；有的块尺度各向异性强、需要自适应缩放；有的块对低精度或去掉动量更耐受。现有 8-bit optimizer、Adafactor、GaLore 等方法通常把同一种优化器配置施加到全模型，容易在某些块上浪费状态显存，在另一些块上损害收敛。","importance":"这篇工作的价值不在提出一个新的优化器，而在把“优化器怎么配”变成了一个可测量、可约束、可自动求解的分配问题。对预训练研究来说，这很实际：优化器状态是随参数量线性增长的长期显存开销，直接挤占 activation、batch size 和 sequence length 的空间。BAOC 给出的思路是只在需要的参数块上保留昂贵状态，把省下的显存换成更大的 batch、更长上下文或更低的并行压力。贡献量级更偏系统化和工程方法论，但切中大规模训练中的真实瓶颈。","technical_approach":"BAOC 将模型参数划分为 N 个不重叠 block，并为每个 block 从候选优化器配置集合中选择一个配置。这里的“配置”包括更新机制和状态精度，例如是否保留动量、是否使用自适应二阶统计、是否低精度存储状态等。流程分四步：第一，稀疏采样梯度流。每个 block 固定采样一小部分坐标，默认采样率 s=0.1%，训练中只记录这些坐标上的梯度序列，避免完整梯度统计带来的额外开销。第二，计算 block 级指标，例如方向稳定性、尺度各向异性和噪声相关信号。这些指标对应优化器机制的需求：方向稳定时动量更可能有效，尺度差异大时自适应缩放更有必要，波动强时便宜配置的风险更高。第三，把这些统计量映射成每个 block-配置组合的 mismatch risk。第四，求解带约束的混合整数线性规划：每个 block 必须选择一个配置，在总 optimizer-state memory 和相对 update time 预算内最小化总风险。论文对 StateMem 的定义比较严格，只统计优化器持有的持久状态张量，不包括模型参数、梯度、activation、临时 workspace、FP32 master weight 或 EMA 权重。时间预算则相对 AdamW16 归一化，并通过轻量 one-step timing 得到候选配置的相对更新成本。和全局低比特优化器或单一 Adafactor/GaLore 的区别是，BAOC 不假设某个便宜优化器适合所有层，而是把机制选择、精度选择和资源预算绑定起来做 block-wise allocation。","experiments":"论文声称在 vision、language 和 diffusion workloads 上验证了 BAOC 的 memory-quality trade-off：在显著降低 optimizer-state memory 的同时维持训练质量。已给出的全文片段没有包含具体实验表格，因此不能可靠复述最终精度、loss、perplexity 或显存节省百分比。可确认的具体数字包括：梯度统计默认只采样每个 block 0.1% 的坐标；时间成本以 AdamW16 的 block 更新耗时为 1 做归一化；优化目标同时受 memory budget 和 time budget 约束。实验部分值得重点核对三类结果：同等显存预算下 BAOC 相比全局 8-bit/Adafactor/GaLore 的质量差距；同等质量下 optimizer-state memory 的节省；风险指标、采样率、block 粒度和 MILP 约束的消融。","reading_guide":"建议精读方法部分的三个点。第一，看 StateMem 定义，因为它决定了论文声称的显存节省是否和实际训练显存一致；这里不包括 activation 和 master weights，解读实验时要分清 optimizer-state memory 与 end-to-end peak memory。第二，看 mismatch risk 如何从梯度统计映射到配置风险，这是方法是否可迁移到 LLM 预训练的关键。第三，看 MILP formulation，包括变量、目标函数、memory/time 约束和候选配置集合。实验部分优先看 memory-quality 曲线、同预算对比、block-wise assignment 可视化和 ablation。若只为 LLM 预训练落地，重点关注 language workload、采样开销、训练早期统计是否稳定，以及配置切换是否会引入 optimizer state warmup 问题。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets optimizer-state memory in large-scale training. Its main claim is that a single global optimizer configuration is often wasteful because gradient dynamics vary across parameter blocks. Some blocks benefit from momentum, some need adaptive scaling due to strong anisotropy, and some can tolerate lower precision or simpler updates. Applying one cheap optimizer everywhere saves memory but can damage sensitive blocks; applying Adam-style states everywhere spends memory where they are not needed.","importance":"The contribution is a practical allocation framework rather than a new optimizer. This matters for pretraining because optimizer states are persistent O(parameter) memory and compete directly with activations, batch size, sequence length, and parallelism choices. BAOC suggests a useful direction: retain expensive optimizer states only where gradient statistics justify them, and use cheaper configurations elsewhere. The impact is mainly methodological and systems-oriented, but it addresses a real scaling bottleneck in foundation-model training.","technical_approach":"$a","experiments":"The supplied text states that BAOC is evaluated on vision, language, and diffusion workloads and achieves competitive memory-quality trade-offs with much lower optimizer-state memory. The provided full-content excerpt does not include the result tables, so exact loss, accuracy, perplexity, or memory-reduction numbers cannot be verified here. The concrete reported settings include a default sparse gradient sampling rate of 0.1%, update-time normalization to AdamW16, and a joint memory/time constrained allocation objective. The most important experimental evidence to inspect is the memory-quality frontier against global low-memory optimizers, quality retention under matched optimizer-state budgets, block-wise assignment patterns, and ablations over sampling rate, risk metrics, block granularity, and time constraints.","reading_guide":"Read the method section closely. The most important details are the optimizer-state memory definition, the gradient-derived risk metrics, and the MILP formulation. These determine whether the approach is merely a bookkeeping tool or a reliable training-time decision procedure. In the experiments, prioritize language-model results, memory-quality curves, assignment visualizations, and ablations on metric quality and sampling overhead. For pretraining use, check whether early gradient samples are stable enough, whether configurations are fixed or updated during training, and how optimizer states are initialized when a block receives a different configuration.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"应立刻看实验细节；若 blockwise optimizer 风险估计稳定，预训练预算分配不该再默认全层 AdamW。","en":"Read the experimental details now; if the blockwise optimizer-risk estimate is stable, full-model AdamW should no longer be the default budget allocation."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Xi’an Jiaotong University"],"authors":["Kang Liu","Wei Peng","Jianchen Hu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["optimizer","training-efficiency","memory-optimization","gradient-dynamics","pretraining"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04711","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.04711","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.04291","title":"Leveraging Pretrained Language Models as Energy Functions for Glauber Dynamics Text Diffusion","abstract":"We present a discrete diffusion-based language model using Glauber dynamics from statistical physics. Our main insight is that instead of trying to train a discrete state space diffusion model using Glauber dynamics with a uniform transition kernel as the forward process, one can set up an ``energy function'' based on pretrained causal/masked language models. When viewed as the stationary distribution, this energy function allows us to significantly improve the quality of the generated text. Inc","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-05T20:51:03+00:00","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2605_04291.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究离散文本 diffusion LM 的一个核心瓶颈：常见 masked diffusion 或离散 diffusion 往往把均匀分布、unigram 分布或 mask-heavy 分布作为噪声端点，这个分布离真实文本太远，导致训练样本效率低、生成质量弱、采样过程需要补很多模型本来可以从预训练中获得的语言知识。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值在于，它把已有 pretrained LM 当成离散 diffusion 的 energy function，而不是只把预训练模型作为初始化或 teacher。这样做改变了 forward process 的设计：扩散链的 stationary distribution 不再是信息量很低的噪声分布，而是由预训练语言模型诱导的、更接近数据分布的能量模型。这个方向对非 AR 语言建模很重要，因为它提供了一条利用既有 AR / masked LM 训练投入来构建 diffusion LM 的路线，避免从均匀噪声到自然语言之间搭一座过长的桥。","technical_approach":"论文采用 Glauber dynamics 构造离散状态空间上的扩散过程。状态空间是长度为 L 的 token 序列。Glauber dynamics 每次局部更新一个位置，并以某个能量函数定义的分布作为 stationary distribution。作者的关键选择是用 pretrained LM 定义能量函数，使目标分布形如 p(x) ∝ exp(-f(x))。相比使用均匀 transition kernel 或 mask-only forward process，这样的噪声端点保留了更多语言结构。\n\n实现上，论文主要使用 UL2 风格模型作为 energy function。UL2 同时覆盖 causal generation、mask infilling 等目标，比纯 GPT-style causal LM 更适合放入需要局部重采样和条件填充的 Glauber diffusion 框架。作者从 UL2 权重初始化，再加入扩散时间相关变量，用适配 Glauber forward process 的 score-entropy loss 训练 diffusion model。附录还讨论了如何使用 GPT-style 模型作为 energy function，但主线选择 UL2，是因为它在双向填充和自回归生成之间提供了更自然的接口。","experiments":"论文报告的实验覆盖语言建模质量、分布相似度、常识推理、结构化推理和 compute-normalized 采样效率。结果包括：无条件生成 perplexity 明显优于已有离散 diffusion LM；generative 和 zero-shot perplexity 接近同规模 AR 模型；MAUVE 分数改善；在 Winogrande、PIQA、SIQA、HellaSwag 等常识任务上相比 prior diffusion models 有竞争力或更好。\n\n结构化推理部分，模型在 Sudoku 和 Zebra puzzle 上优于 masked diffusion LM，并且在不额外加入 token-order robustness 训练的情况下取得这一结果。采样效率实验使用 T5-Gemma 2B–2B backbone，在 reasoning 和 language tasks 上微调。单次 Glauber-UL2 generation 的成本约为 2L 次模型调用；在相同 compute budget 下，N=1 能匹配或超过 AR 模型生成两个独立候选的结果，N=2 时优势进一步扩大。全文片段没有给出具体 perplexity、MAUVE 或 benchmark 数值，因此不能补充精确幅度。","reading_guide":"建议精读 forward Markov process 的定义、Glauber dynamics 的 stationary distribution 设定，以及 score-entropy loss 如何适配该过程。核心问题是为什么好的 base / noisy distribution 会降低 diffusion LM 的学习难度。这里不要只看“用了预训练模型”这一点，要看它是如何进入 Markov chain 的能量函数，而不是只作为参数初始化。\n\n第二个重点是与 MDLM 的比较。论文认为 MDLM 的限制来自 forward process 和 noisy / stationary distribution 的设计，而不只是模型容量或训练技巧。建议看第 5 节中对 MDLM 的批评和对 Glauber dynamics 的动机说明。实验部分优先看三类表：perplexity 与 MAUVE、Sudoku / Zebra puzzle、compute-normalized best-of-N。预计精读需要 1.5 到 2 小时；如果只泛读，重点看 introduction、method 中 energy function 的构造、以及 best-of-N 实验即可。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a central weakness of discrete diffusion language models: many existing methods diffuse toward a uniform, unigram, or mask-dominated distribution that is far from natural text. This makes the reverse process hard to learn, hurts sample efficiency, and leaves the model to relearn linguistic structure that is already available in pretrained language models.","importance":"The main contribution is to use pretrained language models as energy functions for discrete text diffusion, not merely as initialization or teachers. This changes the forward process. The stationary distribution of the Markov chain is no longer a low-information noise distribution; it is an LM-induced distribution that is closer to real text. For non-autoregressive language modeling, this is a useful design point: it reuses the compute already spent on AR or masked pretraining while giving diffusion models a better base distribution than uniform corruption.","technical_approach":"$b","experiments":"$c","reading_guide":"Read the sections defining the forward Markov process, the Glauber stationary distribution, and the score-entropy objective. The key point is how the pretrained LM enters the Markov chain as an energy function, rather than simply providing initial weights. This is where the method differs most from standard masked diffusion language modeling.\n\nThe comparison with MDLMs is also worth reading closely. The paper argues that MDLM limitations come from the choice of forward process and noisy distribution, not only from model scale or training recipes. For experiments, prioritize the perplexity and MAUVE tables, the Sudoku and Zebra puzzle results, and the compute-normalized best-of-N study. 关键构造：把网络前向传播写成“层间动力学”，参数扰动导致的激活扰动满足线性化递推；选择的几何/散度决定 LQR 的二次代价项。这样全局密集二次型被分解为沿层传播的动态系统 + 层级代价，跨层耦合通过动力学自然出现。\n- 精确解：用 Riccati 递推可恢复对应的精确几何更新（作为 reference）。\n- 可扩展松弛（LLQR）：不直接求精确 Riccati，而是把更新参数化为预条件梯度 Δθ = -U g，其中 U 取块对角结构（每层一个块，可选对角、Kronecker-factored 等）。关键点是：先从“密集几何更新”等价得到 layer-coupled 的 LQR 目标，再在该目标下学习/拟合结构化的逆预条件器 U，而不是一开始就把曲率矩阵替换成块对角近似。\n- 工程形态：作为 wrapper 包在 SGDM/AdamW 外，预条件器可跨迭代复用，避免每步显式曲率求逆。","experiments":"实验覆盖 ResNet 与 Transformer，并包含翻译与“grokking 加速”等现象性结果。论文主张 LLQR 在收敛速度与最终指标上优于若干基线，并且额外计算开销“适中”。\n但就预训练研究者关心的可迁移性而言，需要重点核对：\n- Transformer 规模是否足够大、是否包含长序列/大 batch It theoretically differentiates common manifold constraints: spectral spheres regulate worst-case activation scales, while Frobenius spheres control average-case behavior. It verifies manifold constraints can maintain training stability without learnable normalization layers, and their intrinsic geometric updates lock relative learning rates and limit rotation between consecutive weight updates, fulfilling the regularization role of weight decay without heuristic tuning.","experiments":"On standard LLM architectures, MACRO achieves validation loss comparable to or slightly better than the empirical constrained optimizer MuonH, with formal convergence guarantees. We introduce adaptive inverted-index routing for MoE (AIR-MoE), an inverted-index-inspired routing architecture based on vector quantization (VQ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-06T14:15:10+00:00","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2605_04952.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 granular MoE 的路由开销问题。已有 scaling 结果显示，很多小专家往往比少量大专家更有利，但专家数增加后，router 需要对每个 token 和大量 expert centroid 做内积，路由本身会变成主要计算开销。核心问题是：如何在不把专家硬分组、不限制 expert 表示形式的前提下，近似 full top-K routing。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在 MoE 架构可扩展性。它不是提出新的 MoE scaling law，而是试图补上 granular MoE 的系统瓶颈：当专家粒度继续变细时，稀疏激活省下的 FFN 计算可能被路由计算吃掉。AIR-MoE 的贡献量级更接近一种 router 设计和训练方法，适合作为大规模 MoE 预训练中的候选组件。它的关键价值在于把 codeword 对应的预计算 expert shortlist 中取候选专家。第二阶段是 fine scoring：只在 shortlist 内计算 token 与 expert centroid 的精确内积，再选出 top-K experts 做常规 MoE dispatch。这样保留了 top-K routing 的形式，但避免对全部 experts 打分。它和 hierarchical MoE 的区别是，expert 不被固定划分到互斥组里，shortlist 可以重叠；它和结构化 router 的区别是，不要求 expert centroid 满足特殊参数约束。难点在于 codeword 分配不可微。论文采用 bi-level optimization：router 和模型参数仍用梯度下降训练，codebook 用梯度无关的 adaptive spherical k-means 更新，以适应训练过程中 hidden state 分布的漂移。理论部分给出 mass recall 的下界，说明 shortlist 能保留多少原始 top-K routing 概率质量；下界依赖量化误差，以及落在 top-M codeword 分数之外的 routing mass。","experiments":"论文报告 AIR-MoE 在 granular MoE 数量、adaptive k-means 更新策略对 loss 和吞吐的影响。若这些消融成立，说明近似检索式 routing 可以在不牺牲太多 routing flexibility 的情况下支撑更细粒度专家。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20–30 分钟。重点看 Section 3 的 AIR-MoE forward pass 和 Algorithm 1，确认 shortlist Empirical scaling results suggest that many small experts can be preferable to a few large experts, but this regime makes the router evaluate inner products between every token and a large number of expert centroids. The concrete question is how to approximate full top-K routing without forcing experts into fixed groups or imposing restrictive structure on expert representations.","importance":"The relevance for pretraining is in MoE scalability. In contrast to the standard autoregressive next-token prediction based on an exact prefix, we propose a perturbation-based procedure that first transforms the prefix into a semantic neighbor and then conditions on this perturbed variant for next-token prediction. available when the positional generator is non-diagonalizable, with a complex rotary eigenvalue and a nilpotent response placed in the same order-two Jordan block.","importance":"The main value is for long-context positional encoding design. Standard RoPE gives a phase basis, while ALiBi gives an additive distance bias. Many RoPE variants change frequency scaling, interpolation, or damping. Jordan-RoPE instead changes the primitive functions available to the pre-softmax attention logit. It directly exposes d·exp(iωd), a distance-modulated phase feature. This is different from putting distance and phase in separate channels, because the coupling is supplied by the positional representation itself. The contribution is structural rather than an empirical claim about large-scale pretraining.","technical_approach":"The construction uses the view that linear, translation-invariant, continuous positional encodings form one-parameter matrix actions. RoPE corresponds to a diagonalizable complex rotation. Defective generators introduce polynomial factors through Jordan blocks. The paper focuses on the minimal complex defective case: an order-two complex Jordan block. Its matrix exponential contains both the rotary phase exp(iωd) and the tangent feature d·exp(iωd). Because the block is non-orthogonal, the usual symmetric query/key action does not directly yield a pure relative score. The exact version therefore uses a contragredient action on queries. Existing approaches typically handle multi-draft and multi-step aspects in isolation, applying either flat OT to single-step drafts or per-token rejection sampling to tree-structured candidates. This separation leaves the joint regime (where multi-step dependencies meet multi-draft branching) poorly optimized, as local verification rules fail to exp","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-06T06:42:58+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_04543.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 speculative decoding 里一个长期分裂的问题：多 draft 候选的横向选择，和多 step 树结构的纵向依赖，过去通常分别优化，缺少一个同时处理两者且严格保持 lossless 的验证框架。更具体地说，单步 OT 方法只在当前深度做最优分配，不理解后续前缀是否还能继续被接受；而 RRSw 这类树验证方法虽然考虑了多步依赖，但仍按局部、顺序式 rejection sampling 处理兄弟节点，无法在同一层做全局协调。论文的核心问题就是：如何在 candidate tree 上把“横向多候选最优分配”和“纵向前缀接受概率传播”统一起来，在不改变目标模型输出分布的前提下，提升期望接受长度并减少验证过程中的串行依赖。","importance":"对基座研究员来说，这不是预训练方法论文，而是推理算法里较有方法味道的一篇。它的重要性主要有三点。第一，它把 speculative decoding 的若干变体放进了一个统一的 conditional OT 视角里，解释了为什么单步 OT 和树式 rejection sampling 各自只解决了一半问题。第二，它给出了一种可证明的条件最优性结论：在给定前缀接受约束下，Greedy 采样策略配合其分配规则可以达到理论上界，并且这个上界不弱于单步 Greedy OT。第三，它把树验证从顺序式 RRSw 改写成更并行的单次规划与后序决策流程，这对高吞吐 serving 有直接意义。量级上看，它不是范式替换，而是对 speculative decoding 理论和实现边界的一次扎实推进。","technical_approach":"方法的关键是引入 prefix acceptance probability 作为跨层耦合变量。直观地说，某个节点的子节点能分到多少“接受质量”，不只取决于当前 target/draft 分布的重叠，还取决于这个父前缀本身有多大概率能走到。过去单步 OT 默认每层都在完整概率空间里做分配，忽略了这种前缀缩放；而 RRSw 虽然隐式考虑了前缀是否存活，但它通过逐个候选重归一化来实现，导致兄弟节点之间缺少联合最优。\n\nUniVer 把树验证拆成两个阶段。第一阶段是 top-down allocation：从根开始，把每个节点的前缀接受概率向下传递，并在每一层把对子节点的接受分配写成 conditional OT 问题。这里的“conditional”含义是，子节点的可接受质量受父节点前缀接受概率约束，因此每层不是独立 OT，而是在动态缩放后的条件概率空间里求最优匹配。第二阶段是 post-order decision：在已经算好的接受 plan 上，自底向上或后序地做实际接受/拒绝决策，保证最终采样过程与目标模型分布一致。\n\n论文强调，相比 RRSw 的 sequential dependency，UniVer 可以对同一深度的所有分支并行计算 acceptance plan，因为它不再依赖“拒绝一个候选后再重算下一个候选”的局部流程，而是先在统一概率空间里把兄弟节点的接受质量一次性分好。理论部分给出的核心结论是：在 Greedy draft selection 设定下，对任意前缀接受概率 p_v∈(0,1]，UniVer 达到该前缀约束下的条件最优接受上界；并且当树退化为单层时，它自然退化到已有的单步 OT Greedy 方法。也就是说，这不是另起炉灶的启发式，而是把单步 OT 最优性推广到了树结构。\n\n从方法取舍看，这篇工作没有去碰 draft model 训练，也没有改 target verification kernel，而是专注于 verification rule 本身。这样做的好处是 lossless 性质清楚，能直接替换现有树式 speculative decoding 的验证模块；代价是收益上限仍受 draft 质量和树构造方式约束。","experiments":"实验结论比较集中，重点看 acceptance length 而不是端到端 wall-clock。论文在多种 LLM 与不同 tree architecture 上比较了 UniVer、Vanilla RRSw、Traversal，以及单步 OT 的 Greedy 类方法。主结果是：在 temperature 1.0 下，UniVer 相对标准 RRSw 的 acceptance length 提升约 4.2% 到 8.5%；摘要里给出的上界是最高 8.5%。同时，它稳定优于只做单步横向优化的 Greedy method，说明纵向前缀约束确实带来了额外收益，而不是简单复现单步 OT 的结果。\n\n更有信息量的实验点在于几类对比。第一，树越深、分支越多时，UniVer 相对局部验证规则的优势更明显，因为这时横向与纵向耦合更强，局部规则更容易浪费接受质量。第二，在保持 lossless 的前提下，UniVer 的单次规划减少了 RRSw 那种逐候选递归处理带来的串行性，因此实现上更适合并行。第三，理论与实验是对齐的：当结构退化到单步场景时，UniVer 不会比单步 OT 更差；当存在真实树依赖时，它能超过单步方法。\n\n从摘要和引言可确认的数字还包括：提升区间 4.2%–8.5%，比较温度为 1.0，且论文声称在各种模型和树结构上结果一致。若精读全文，建议把具体模型规模、draft branching factor、tree depth、以及 acceptance length 如何换算成实际 speedup 一并核实，因为这决定了工程价值的上限。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓住三个点，不必一开始陷进证明细节。第一，看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，弄清作者说的 horizontal vs. vertical trade-off 到底是什么，为什么单步 OT 与 RRSw 各有盲区。第二，看方法部分里 prefix acceptance probability 的定义，以及 conditional OT 的构造方式。这是整篇的核心，如果这里没看懂，后面的最优性证明和实验都容易变成表面结论。第三，看实验表里不同 tree 结构下的 acceptance length 提升，判断收益是否主要来自特定配置。\n\n如果时间有限，建议阅读顺序是：Introduction → Preliminaries 中对 Greedy / RRSw 的回顾 → UniVer 主算法 → 理论命题/定理 → 主实验。预计 30–45 分钟可以建立完整印象。更值得深挖的点有两个：一是 conditional OT 的目标和约束是否还能推广到更一般的 draft proposal，而不只依赖 Greedy selection；二是 acceptance length 的提升在真实系统里能否稳定转成 latency 改善，尤其当 verification kernel、KV 复用和 batch 调度进入系统瓶颈时。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a structural gap in speculative decoding: prior methods optimize either horizontal selection across multiple drafts at one step, or vertical coordination across multiple steps in a tree, but not both jointly. Single-step OT-based verification is optimal only within one depth and ignores whether a prefix is likely to survive into deeper levels. Tree-based methods such as RRSw account for prefix dependencies, but they verify siblings through local sequential rejection rules rather than a globally coordinated allocation. The core problem is therefore how to verify a draft tree under both branching and depth dependencies, while remaining strictly lossless with respect to the target model distribution.","importance":"For foundation-model researchers, this is not a pretraining paper, but it is a solid inference-method paper with a clean conceptual contribution. Its value comes from three aspects. First, it gives a unified conditional-OT view that explains why multi-draft OT and multi-step rejection sampling each solve only part of the problem. Second, it proves a conditional optimality result: under prefix acceptance constraints and a Greedy draft selection strategy, UniVer reaches the theoretical upper bound on acceptance and is never worse than the single-step Greedy OT baseline. Third, it turns tree verification from a sequential recursive procedure into a more parallelizable allocation-and-decision pipeline. This is not a new decoding paradigm, but it is a meaningful refinement of the theory and implementation boundary of speculative decoding.","technical_approach":"$f","experiments":"$10","reading_guide":"$11","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看条件 OT 形式化和验证伪代码即可；若加速收益稳定，可作为 speculative decoding 内部实现的候选替换。","en":"Read the conditional-OT formulation and verification pseudocode; if the speedups are stable, this is a plausible drop-in candidate for speculative decoding infrastructure."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Lenovo AI Technology Center"],"authors":["Yepeng Weng","Qiao Hu","Takehisa Yairi"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["speculative-decoding","optimal-transport","inference-efficiency","tree-verification"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04543","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.04543","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.04075","title":"RetentiveKV: State-Space Memory for Uncertainty-Aware Multimodal KV Cache Eviction","abstract":"Multimodal Large Language Models face severe challenges in computational efficiency and memory consumption due to the substantial expansion of the visual KV cache when processing long visual contexts. The key idea behind Coral is to join","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-05T23:25:29+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_04357.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.DC","cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决多模型 LLM 在线服务中的联合优化问题：在云上 GPU 类型、价格、地域可用性不断变化的情况下，同时决定购买哪些异构节点，以及每个模型副本如何切分和放置，目标是在满足吞吐和延迟 SLO 的前提下降低成本。","importance":"这不是预训练方法论文，但对基座模型研究有工程参考价值。模型族和模型尺寸越来越分散，训练团队在评估模型发布形态时，不能只看单模型质量，也要考虑 serving 可部署性、异构硬件适配和多模型组合成本。Coral 的启发在于把模型 placement 从在线决策中拆出来，形成可缓存的 Serving Template。这类思路也会影响预训练阶段的架构取舍，例如模型大小、层宽、KV cache 占用、prefill/decode 比例，以及是否容易在非同构 GPU 上切分运行。","technical_approach":"Coral 将问题拆成离线和在线两阶段。离线阶段针对每个 model 和 latency SLO，枚举一组有代表性的节点组合，并用 ILP 求解该组合上的吞吐最优模型放置方案。求得的结果被缓存为 Serving Template。关键观察是：给定模型、SLO 和节点组合后，最优 placement 只依赖这个组合，不依赖其他模型如何占用集群。因此 placement 可以离线复用。在线阶段只需要用轻量 ILP 在不同 region、GPU 价格和可用性约束下选择模板，满足各模型的实时吞吐需求并最小化成本。系统实现使用 ZeroMQ 和 NCCL，单节点执行引擎基于 vLLM，同时构建了事件驱动模拟器来做大规模评估。","experiments":"评估覆盖 6 个模型、5 类 GPU、20 种 GPU 配置，并考虑资源可用性和吞吐需求变化。论文报告 Coral 相比最强 baseline 最高降低 2.79 倍 serving cost；在资源稀缺场景下，最高获得 2.39 倍 goodput。在线求解时间从直接联合搜索可能需要的小时级降到几十秒级，使系统能够跟上价格、可用性和请求量变化。论文还强调，已有方法如果把每个模型副本当作 homogeneous black box，会漏掉大量异构 placement 的成本空间；反过来，如果只做单模型异构 placement，再在外层枚举资源分配，搜索开销不可接受。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25 到 35 分钟。优先看问题形式化、Serving Template 的定义、离线 ILP 与在线 ILP 的分解理由，以及资源稀缺场景的评估。对预训练研究员来说，不必深挖系统实现细节；更值得关注的是异构 serving 约束如何反过来影响模型架构和模型规格设计。若关心推理成本建模，可以看 vLLM/NCCL runtime 与 simulator 的校准部分。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses joint optimization for serving many LLMs on heterogeneous cloud GPUs. Given per-model demand, latency SLOs, real-time GPU prices, and regional availability, the system must decide which nodes to provision and how to place each model replica across those nodes at minimum cost.","importance":"This is an inference-serving paper rather than a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to foundation-model engineering. Model providers increasingly serve many model families and sizes at the same time, and cloud supply is heterogeneous. The result is that model quality alone is not enough; deployability across mixed GPUs also matters. Coral’s template-based decomposition is useful for thinking about how architecture choices affect serving cost: parameter count, layer shape, KV-cache footprint, prefill/decode balance, and the ease of tensor or pipeline partitioning across non-identical hardware.","technical_approach":"Coral decomposes the joint problem into an offline placement stage and an online allocation stage. Offline, for each model and latency SLO, it enumerates a selected set of node combinations and solves an ILP to find the throughput-optimal placement on each combination. The resulting artifact is a Serving Template. The key observation is that, once the model, SLO, and node combination are fixed, the best placement does not depend on how the rest of the cluster is allocated. Online, Coral solves a smaller ILP that selects templates across regions under current price and availability constraints to satisfy demand at minimum cost. The implementation uses ZeroMQ and NCCL, with vLLM as the per-node execution engine. The authors also build an event-based simulator for larger-scale evaluation.","experiments":"The evaluation covers 6 models, 5 GPU types, and 20 GPU configurations under changing demand and resource availability. Coral reduces serving cost by up to 2.79× over the strongest baseline. Under scarce availability, it delivers up to 2.39× higher goodput. The online solve runs in tens of seconds, compared with the hours-level cost implied by direct joint search or by wrapping single-model placement solvers inside an outer allocation enumeration. The experiments support the main design claim: treating each model replica as a homogeneous black box leaves cost savings unused, while solving full placement online is too slow for changing cloud conditions.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25 to 35 minutes. We present FASQ (Flexible Accelerated Subspace Quantization), a calibration-free framework that applies product quantization to LLM weight matrices. By tuning two parameters, sub-vector size and codebook cardinality, FASQ exposes a co","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-22T20:03:36+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_04084.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.AR"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在不依赖校准数据的前提下，把 LLM 权重压缩从“固定 bit-width 的少数离散点”扩展为“可连续调节的压缩—精度折中”，同时避免非标准 bit-width 下标量量化需要专用 kernel/反量化带来的推理延迟问题。","importance":"对基座预训练本身的直接贡献不大（属于部署侧 PTQ/内核工程），但对“训练—部署闭环”有现实意义：1）提供了校准数据不可得时的可用压缩路径（私有数据/合规场景）；2）用 PQ 的“查表直接算”替代“反量化再 GEMM”，把压缩点从 8/4/3-bit 的离散选择变成连续设计空间，减少工程上为每个 bit-width 维护不同 kernel 的压力；3）结果显示在某些压缩率下吞吐可超过 FP16，提示推理瓶颈从算力转向带宽时，权重表示方式会反过来决定端到端效率。","technical_approach":"方法核心是把线性层权重矩阵做多码本 Product Quantization（PQ）。将每个权重向量切分为若干 sub-vector（由 sub-vector size 决定），每段用 k-means 学到的 codebook（由 codebook cardinality 决定）进行编码；存储为“码本 + uint8 索引表”，从而在 27%–49% FP16 体积范围内提供细粒度压缩点。\n推理侧避免显式反量化：\n- decode（GEMV）阶段用 LUT-free 的 direct-compute kernel：在寄存器里做 centroid-dot-product 累加，减少共享内存查表开销；\n- prefill（GEMM）阶段用 split-K + LUT 的 kernel：码本缓存到 shared memory，做 table-lookup-and-accumulate，提高 occupancy。\n与 GPTQ/AWQ 的关键差异：FASQ 不用校准集（不估 Hessian/activation 统计），误差控制主要来自 PQ 的向量量化能力；与标量量化不同，压缩率不是 bit-width 的离散点，而由（sub-vector size, codebook size）连续调参得到；并且计算路径不依赖“先解码成 FP16/INT 再 matmul”的模式。","experiments":"论文在三类 8–9B 模型上报告一致趋势：Meta-Llama-3-8B、Qwen3-8B、Qwen3.5-9B-Base。\n- 质量：在 Llama-3-8B 上，49% FP16 体积时平均准确率约 68.2（作者称接近 8-bit 质量）；在 37%–42% 体积区间，平均准确率约 67.1–67.7，优于/不弱于 4-bit GPTQ 与 AWQ（同区间对比）。\n- 吞吐与延迟：RTX 3090 上 decode 吞吐 45.2 tok/s（优于 FP16 的 43.9 tok/s），且在更高压缩（作者称“有效 3-bit”）时可到 51.8 tok/s；相对 AWQ 提升约 1.6–1.8×，相对 GPTQ 约 2.2–2.5×，相对 RTN 约 4.3–5.0×。作者强调 decode 延迟随压缩提升而改善，和标量量化在非标准 bit-width 下常见的 kernel/反量化开销相反。\n消融/设计空间部分的关键信息是：sub-vector size 与 codebook cardinality 共同决定压缩率与误差，且能“填补”INT8 与 INT4 之间的空档。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 20–30 分钟）：\n1）先看 Introduction size, codebook size）下的模型体积与精度曲线，判断它是否真的提供“连续可用”的中间点。\n4）对比表里注意对齐条件：是否同任务集、同推理设置、同 batch/seq 长度；PQ 方法对序列长度与 batch 的敏感性往往比标量量化更强，读结果时要确认评测配置。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Enable calibration-free LLM weight compression with a continuous compression–accuracy trade-off (beyond fixed 8/4/3-bit scalar quantization points), while keeping inference fast by avoiding dequantization and the need for bespoke kernels at non-standard bit-widths.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper; it sits in post-training quantization and kernel engineering. Still, it matters to foundation-model practitioners because (1) it offers a viable path when calibration data is unavailable (privacy/compliance/proprietary domains), (2) it turns compression from a few discrete operating points into a tunable design space, reducing the engineering burden of supporting many bit-width-specific kernels, and (3) it highlights a regime where memory bandwidth dominates and a compressed representation with direct compute can outperform FP16 throughput.","technical_approach":"FASQ applies multi-codebook Product Quantization (PQ) to LLM linear-layer weight matrices. Weights are partitioned into sub-vectors; each sub-vector is encoded by an index into a learned k-means codebook. Storage becomes “codebooks + uint8 index tables”, and the compression ratio is controlled by two knobs: sub-vector size and codebook cardinality, yielding 27%–49% of FP16 size with fine granularity.\nInference is performed directly on the compressed form:\n- Decode (GEMV): a LUT-free direct-compute CUDA kernel accumulates centroid dot-products in registers, avoiding shared-memory lookup overhead.\n- Prefill (GEMM): a split-K LUT-based kernel caches codebooks in shared memory and performs lookup-and-accumulate to improve occupancy.\nKey differences vs GPTQ/AWQ: no calibration set (no Hessian/activation statistics), vector quantization rather than scalar rounding, and no explicit dequantization to FP16/INT prior to matmul.","experiments":"Reported on Meta-Llama-3-8B, Qwen3-8B, and Qwen3.5-9B-Base with consistent trends.\n- Accuracy (Llama-3-8B): ~68.2 average accuracy at 49% FP16 size (claimed near INT8 quality). At 37%–42% size, ~67.1–67.7 average accuracy, outperforming or matching 4-bit GPTQ/AWQ in that range.\n- Throughput/latency (RTX 3090): decode throughput 45.2 tok/s vs FP16 43.9 tok/s, and up to 51.8 tok/s at higher compression (described as “effective 3-bit”). Relative decode throughput gains: ~1.6–1.8× over AWQ, ~2.2–2.5× over GPTQ, ~4.3–5.0× over RTN. A notable claim is that decode latency improves as compression increases, unlike scalar quantization where non-standard bit-widths often incur dequantization/kernel penalties.\nThe design-space exploration ties (sub-vector size, codebook size) to both model size and quality, aiming to fill the INT8–INT4 gap with usable intermediate points.","reading_guide":"Skim plan (20–30 minutes):\n1) Read the Introduction carefully for the three deployment pain points and why PQ addresses each.\n2) Focus on the two kernel sections: why LUT-free GEMV helps decode, and how split-K LUT GEMM sustains occupancy in prefill.\n3) Inspect the compression–accuracy curves/tables across (sub-vector size, codebook cardinality) to verify the “continuous operating points” claim.\n4) When reading comparisons, check evaluation alignment (tasks, batch/seq length, kernel settings). PQ-style methods can be more sensitive to sequence length and batching than scalar quantization.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"不是 pretrain 论文，但若你关心 serving 约束反推模型设计，这篇值得看 kernel 部分；精度结论先当中等强度信号，重点在连续压缩空间。","en":"Not a pretraining paper, but worth a skim if deployment constraints feed back into model design; focus on the kernels and the continuous compression tradeoff, not just the headline accuracy."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of California, Irvine","University of California, IrvineIrvine"],"authors":["Ye Qiao","Yian Wang","Zhiheng Chen","Hyoukjun Kwon","Sitao Huang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["quantization","product-quantization","llm-compression","cuda-kernels","inference"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04084","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.04084","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.05023","title":"CuBridge: An LLM-Based Framework for Understanding and Reconstructing High-Performance Attention Kernels","abstract":"Efficient CUDA implementations of attention mechanisms are critical to modern deep learning systems, yet supporting diverse and evolving attention variants remains challenging. Recent work explores large language models (LLMs) for GPU kernel generation, but prior studies report unstable correctness and significant performance gaps for complex opera","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-06T15:19:07+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_05023.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是高性能 attention CUDA kernel 难以随 attention 变体快速演进的问题。标准框架表达灵活但性能不足，专家手写 kernel 性能好但修改成本高，直接让 LLM 从零生成复杂 attention kernel 又容易出现正确性和性能不稳定。","importance":"它不属于预训练算法论文，但和大模型训练效率相关。长上下文、稀疏 attention、自定义 mask、非标准 normalization、融合流水线都会带来 kernel 维护压力。CuBridge 的启发是：LLM 更适合在有结构约束和可验证中间表示的流程里做语义迁移，而不是直接生成底层 CUDA。对预训练系统来说，这类工具可以降低新 attention 机制从想法到高性能实现的周期。","technical_approach":"CuBridge 采用 lift–transfer–lower 流程。第一步 lift：把专家手写 CUDA attention kernel 提升到可执行的中间表示 CuIR。CuIR 显式表达 execution orchestration，包括线程块、warp、共享内存、寄存器、同步、异步拷贝和 pipeline 等性能关键结构，同时抽象掉部分 CUDA 语法细节。第二步 transfer：根据用户指定的 attention 语义修改 IR，例如改 score function、mask rule、normalization behavior 或 fused pipeline。第三步 lower：参考原始高性能实现，把修改后的 IR 重构回优化 CUDA kernel。关键区别是，它不是让 LLM 从空白代码开始写 kernel，而是保留专家 kernel 的执行结构，只让 LLM 在受控表示上做语义改写。由于 CuIR 可执行，每个阶段都可以做验证，减少错误传播。","experiments":"论文在 NVIDIA A100 和 H100 上评估多种 attention 变体和真实模型配置。结果显示，CuBridge 生成的 kernel 保持正确性，并且平均比通用框架快 16.03 倍，比 template-based compiler 快 1.39 倍，比已有 LLM-based kernel 方法快 3.33 倍。这个结果说明主要收益不是来自 LLM 自身的代码能力，而是来自把专家 kernel 的低层执行结构保留下来，同时让语义变化发生在可验证 IR 中。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20 分钟。先看 Introduction，明确它为什么不走端到端代码生成。然后看 CuIR 设计部分，重点确认 IR 如何表示 execution orchestration，以及哪些 CUDA 细节被保留。实验部分优先看 attention variants 覆盖范围、A100/H100 上的性能对比、正确性验证流程，以及和 template compiler、LLM kernel generation 的失败模式差异。若只关心预训练效率，可重点读与 attention mask、normalization、fused pipeline 相关的例子。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses the difficulty of adapting high-performance CUDA attention kernels to new attention variants. Long-context models and new attention variants often require custom masks, modified score functions, alternative normalization, or fused execution pipelines. CuBridge suggests a practical pattern: use LLMs for constrained semantic adaptation over a verifiable intermediate representation, rather than asking them to generate low-level CUDA from scratch. For pretraining infrastructure, this can shorten the path from a new attention idea to a performant implementation.","technical_approach":"CuBridge follows a lift–transfer–lower workflow. It first lifts expert-written CUDA attention kernels into CuIR, an executable intermediate representation that exposes execution orchestration: block and warp organization, memory hierarchy usage, synchronization, asynchronous copies, and pipelined execution. It then transfers the lifted program to match user-specified attention semantics, such as changes in score computation, masking, normalization, or fused pipelines. Finally, it lowers the transformed IR back into optimized CUDA code using reference-guided reconstruction. The key design choice is to preserve the execution structure encoded by expert kernels and let the LLM operate on a structured representation where semantic edits can be verified.","experiments":"The evaluation covers NVIDIA A100 and H100 GPUs, multiple attention variants, and real-model configurations. MLLM 与 MMDiT 通过共享接口耦合起来，并围绕空间数据、长文本渲染、空间编辑、多视角推理设计训练流程，目标是让理解信号真正反哺生成与编辑。对做 native multimodal pretrain 的人，这类工作有两个启发：一是统一模型不能只靠“同一个 backbone + 多个头”，还需要任务间可传递的中间表示；二是空间能力不是纯 benchmark 指标，而是决定统一模型是否能向 world model、VLA 这类方向延展的基础能力。论文的重要性中等偏上，但从给出的全文片段看，更像一套大规模系统工程与数据配方总结，而不是提出全新的预训练范式。","technical_approach":"整体架构由三部分组成：空间增强的 MLLM、VAE、以及负责图像生成与编辑的 MMDiT。MLLM 被定义为“认知中枢”，既做图像理解和空间推理，也负责解析用户指令、参考图像和编辑意图，并把这些信息转成下游生成模块可消费的条件表示。VAE 负责像素空间与潜变量空间之间的桥接，MMDiT 则在潜空间里完成高保真生成与编辑。\n\n方法上的关键不只是把理解模型和 diffusion 模型串起来，而是让二者通过共享的多模态接口形成双向协作。作者强调 understanding-driven generation：生成和编辑不是直接吃原始文本提示，而是吃经过 MLLM 解析后的、带有语义和空间 grounding 的条件信号。反过来，几何相关的生成与编辑任务，如空间操作、视角扩展、布局变换，也被当成对理解模块的补充监督，让模型在训练中接触“如果场景结构改变，视觉证据会怎样变化”这类信号。\n\n训练上采用多阶段 curriculum。全文片段里能确认的几类数据包括：通用视觉理解数据、细粒度空间推理数据、高保真文生图数据、长文本排版与渲染监督、通用编辑数据、空间编辑数据，以及与 novel-view 或 view-assisted reasoning 相关的数据。这个配方的逻辑是先保证广泛视觉能力，再逐步注入空间约束更强、控制要求更高的任务，避免统一训练时生成目标和理解目标相互干扰。\n\n如果和已有统一模型相比，这篇工作的差异点主要在两处。第一，它把空间 intelligence 作为贯穿式训练目标，而不是在编辑或 grounding 子任务上局部增强。第二，它强调 bidirectional loop：不是单向地用理解指导生成，而是让生成式变换反过来提供空间推理监督。这个思路与一些 AR+diffusion 联合建模工作相近，但这里仍是模块化接口耦合，不是 token-level 的完全统一序列建模。","experiments":"从提供的全文片段可以确认，实验覆盖理解、生成、长文本渲染、编辑，以及空间相关能力，作者声称在 spatial understanding、long-text rendering、multi-view generation 和 controllable editing 上表现较强。但片段中没有给出具体 benchmark 名称、模型规模、训练数据规模、参数量或明确的数值提升，因此无法像精读论文那样复述关键数字。\n\n即便如此，实验设计意图是清楚的：作者不是只测传统图文理解或文生图质量，而是把空间能力拆成多个可观察维度，包括细粒度空间理解、几何精确编辑、长文本排版、以及借助视角变化进行推理。这种评测组织方式本身有参考意义，因为它在检验统一模型是否真的形成了共享空间表征，而不是分别在几个任务上做局部适配。\n\n如果后续阅读全文，最需要确认的是三类结果。第一，统一训练是否真的优于把 MLLM 和 diffusion 模块分开训练再拼接。第二，空间数据和空间编辑监督分别贡献了多少。第三，长文本渲染、多视角生成和空间推理之间是否存在可量化的迁移收益。如果论文在这些点上给出扎实消融，它的说服力会明显提高；如果只是大而全 benchmark 罗列，研究含金量会打折。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和 Figure 4，把系统拓扑理清：MLLM 在哪里结束，MMDiT 从哪里开始，二者共享的接口到底传什么。然后重点看训练数据与多阶段训练章节，确认空间 intelligence 是通过哪些任务和监督注入的。最后看实验中与 spatial understanding、editing、multi-view、long-text rendering 相关的主表和消融。预计 30 分钟左右可以判断这篇工作更偏“系统配方”还是“方法创新”。\n\n阅读时建议抓四个问题。第一，统一接口是浅层 prompt 级耦合，还是包含更结构化的中间表示。第二，空间增强到底来自数据规模，还是来自架构与训练目标设计。第三，生成任务是否真的给理解带来反馈，而不只是共享 backbone 带来的参数复用。第四，长文本渲染和空间编辑是否共享同一类布局表征，这关系到模型是否学到了更一般的二维结构建模能力。\n\n如果时间有限，可以优先看模型图、训练 recipe 总表、以及空间相关消融。若这些部分没有把“为什么这样统一，而不是简单串联现成 MLLM 和 diffusion bilevel optimization。RM 在 policy 诱导的数据分布上更新，policy 的真实目标不是最大化固定 RM 的即时奖励，而是最大化经过 RM retraining dynamics 后的长期有效奖励。作者通过解析展开下层 RM 更新，得到 policy 的真实优化梯度分解：一部分是标准 policy gradient，另一部分是 parameter-steering term。后者刻画 policy 当前样本如何影响未来 RM 参数。这个项通常涉及 bilevel optimization 里的 Hessian inverse 或 influence-function 风格计算，直接做成本很高。\n\n方法上，作者提出 Foresighted Policy Optimization（FPO）。它不是重写整个 RLHF pipeline，而是在 policy optimization 阶段加入一个针对性的 penalty，用来恢复被 PPO/REINFORCE 忽略的 steering 效应。论文先在 pointwise reward setting 中分析几何机制，再扩展到标准 pairwise preference comparison。为了让方法能在 LLM 上运行，FPO 用 TracIn 风格的一阶近似替代精确的二阶 Hessian 明显失真的区域。第二类是较真实的 LLM alignment pipeline，使用 Llama-3.2-1B 进行验证。论文报告 FPO 在该设置中能降低 reward hacking，并使高 RM 分数与真实质量之间的偏离变小。\n\n给出的全文内容没有包含具体表格数值、胜率、KL、reward gap 或 human/LLM judge 细项，因此不能可靠补充精确提升幅度。可确认的规模信息是 hacking 的抑制是否来自 penalty 本身，而不是更强 KL 或更保守采样。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a feedback loop in iterative RLHF that is usually hidden by the standard training abstraction. The policy generates the data used to retrain the reward model, so the current policy also shapes the future parameters of the reward model. Standard PPO- or REINFORCE-style RLHF optimizes the current proxy reward and ignores this parameter-steering effect. The authors call the resulting failure mode alignment collapse: the policy repeatedly exploits reward-model blind spots, obtains high proxy reward with low true utility, and then induces new feedback data that reinforces the same errors.","importance":"The main value is a training-dynamics explanation of reward hacking in iterative RLHF. The paper does not treat reward over-optimization as only an empirical pathology. It derives the missing term in the optimization objective. A foresighted policy should optimize an effective reward composed of the current proxy reward plus a parameter-steering term induced by future RM retraining. This matters beyond RLHF. Any pipeline where a model generates data that later trains a judge, filter, reward model, or data selector can inherit the same closed-loop instability, including RLAIF, self-training, synthetic data bootstrapping, and continual pretraining with model-based filtering.","technical_approach":"$16","experiments":"$17","reading_guide":"$18","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇会直接改变你看 iterative RLHF 的 prior：别再把 RM 当静态 oracle，先读梯度分解和 collapse 条件，再决定内部循环训练要不要加反操纵约束。","en":"This should update your prior on iterative RLHF: stop treating the RM as a static oracle; read the gradient decomposition and collapse mechanism before trusting any closed-loop alignment pipeline."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Inria","Ecole Normale Supérieure","PSL Research University","University of California, Berkeley","Inria, Ecole Normale Supérieure, PSL Research University","Departments of EECS and Statistics, University of California, Berkeley"],"authors":["Etienne Gauthier","Francis Bach","Michael I. typically backpropagates task gradients through the entire Transformer stack, which increases activation memory, creates long backward dependencies, and lets narrow post-training supervision directly perturb broadly useful pretrained representations. The paper asks whether “how far task gradients are allowed to reach” can be treated as an explicit design knob, reducing cost and interference without simply freezing early layers.","importance":"The main contribution is reframing post-training efficiency and representation interference around gradient reach rather than only the number of trainable parameters. A single stop-gradient boundary provides a simple structural lever to cut the backward graph depth, with claimed gains in memory/throughput and better retention of pretrained capabilities. For pretraining researchers, it sharpens the pretrain↔posttrain interface question: narrow supervision can be prevented from rewriting lower-layer features by controlling gradient accessibility, while still allowing limited co-adaptation via local objectives.","technical_approach":"LoPT inserts one detach() at the Transformer midpoint, splitting the model into a first-half block (k1) and a second-half block (k2). k2 is optimized with the standard task objective (token NLL for SFT, policy-gradient-style objective for GRPO). k1 receives no task gradient; instead it is updated with a lightweight feature-reconstruction local loss.\nThe local loss is not meant to solve the downstream task. Its role is to keep k1 trainable and to preserve a stable, pretrained-like representation interface that remains compatible with the adapting k2. Compared to freezing early layers, LoPT allows k1 to adjust, but only under a reconstruction signal rather than narrow task gradients. Compared to PEFT/LoRA, LoPT changes gradient flow (backward depth) rather than parameterization, and is intended to be orthogonal/stackable with LoRA, checkpointing, ZeRO, and pipeline parallelism.","experiments":"The provided full-text excerpt does not include the experimental section, tables, or numeric results, so specific model sizes, data budgets, and measured deltas cannot be quoted reliably. From the introduction, the authors claim: (1) competitive task performance under both SFT and GRPO, (2) reduced training memory and improved efficiency, and (3) better preservation of pretrained capabilities. These claims should be validated by checking: sensitivity to the split point, the exact reconstruction target and weighting, apples-to-apples compute/token-budget comparisons against full end-to-end, freezing, and LoRA baselines, and the evaluation protocol for “capability retention.”","reading_guide":"Read in the order: motivation → method instantiation → ablations.\n- Focus on how the paper defines and motivates “gradient reach” and ties it to activation storage and interference.\n- In the method, pin down what is reconstructed (which features), whether a teacher/EMA target is used, and how the loss is weighted to avoid destabilizing k2 adaptation.\n- In experiments, prioritize comparisons against freezing (same gradient blocking but no k1 updates), LoRA-only, and combined LoPT+LoRA, plus any retention benchmarks and memory accounting methodology. Standard GRPO uses sequence aggregation, while recent work has advocated token aggregation as a better alternative. We show tha","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-14T09:48:46+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_04077.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文指出 GRPO/RLVR 中 token-level policy gradient 如何聚合不是实现细节：token aggregation 会产生正负样本长度耦合，sequence aggregation 又会系统性压低长回答的 token 权重。","importance":"这篇对 post-train 训练动态有直接价值，也对预训练研究有间接启发。RLVR 正在成为推理和代码能力提升的主要接口，而 GRPO 的损失聚合决定了哪些 token、哪些长度的回答真正驱动更新。论文的贡献不是提出新奖励，也不是改采样策略，而是把一个容易被忽略的 loss reduction 选择形式化为优化偏差问题。这个结论对复现实验、比较 DAPO/Dr.GRPO 类方法、分析 length bias 和 reward hacking 都有用。","technical_approach":"论文先把 GRPO 目标拆开：每个 prompt 采样一组回答，按可验证奖励得到组内归一化 advantage，同一条回答的所有 token 共享同一个 sequence-level advantage。问题出在聚合层。Token aggregation 直接对组内所有 token 求平均，因此长回答拥有更多 token 权重。如果正样本和负样本长度分布不同，梯度方向会被长度差放大或削弱，这就是 sign-length coupling。Sequence aggregation 先在每条回答内部平均，再对回答平均，可以消除正负长度耦合，但它让每条回答权重相同，导致长回答的每个 token 被隐式降权。Balanced Aggregation 的折中方式是：按 advantage 符号把组内回答分成正样本和负样本，分别在每个符号子集内部做 token-level 平均，再按正负子集中的 sequence 数量比例合并两个 loss。这样保留了 sequence aggregation 的正负平衡，又避免把每条回答强行等权。","experiments":"实验覆盖 Qwen2.5-Math-7B 和 Qwen3-1.7B 两个模型，训练集包括 DAPO 和 更稳定，最终表现也更好。片段中没有给出完整分数表，因此不补写具体提升数值。最有信息量的实验分析是两个因素：组内回答长度方差，以及正负样本平均长度差。论文认为 token aggregation 在长度方差较大时可能有利，因为它不会过度压低长回答；但当正负长度不平衡时，它更容易产生不稳定更新。","reading_guide":"泛读约 20 分钟。先看 Introduction 中对 token aggregation 和 sequence aggregation 两类偏差的定义。然后看 Method 里的公式，尤其是 sequence-level advantage 与 token-level PPO term 之间的错位，这是问题来源。实验部分重点看三块：BA 与两种基线的训练曲线；按 response length variance 和 positive-negative length gap 分桶的分析；数学与代码任务是否呈现一致趋势。实现 GRPO 或复现 RLVR 训练时，这篇应作为 loss reduction 检查清单使用。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper shows that aggregation in GRPO-style RLVR is not a harmless implementation choice: token aggregation couples gradient sign with response length, while sequence aggregation implicitly downweights tokens in longer responses.","importance":"This is directly relevant to post-training dynamics and indirectly useful for pretraining researchers who track the pretrain-to-RL interface. RLVR has become a common path for improving reasoning and coding models, and the aggregation rule determines which responses and tokens dominate the update. The contribution is not a new reward or sampler. provides uniformly better local learning signals at every intermediate step. The paper shows this assumption is frequently violated in multi-step reasoning and proposes to reframe distillation as token-level learning-signal allocation based on local validity rather than strict path alignment.","importance":"Primarily a post-training/distillation contribution, with two broader implications relevant to pretraining-adjacent pipelines: (1) teacher strength does not translate into uniformly reliable token-level supervision, which matters when using synthetic CoT traces for mid-training or continual pretraining; (2) it offers a general credit-assignment view—calibrating update magnitude per decision point under a local validity signal—compatible with KL-regularized training and other trust-region style objectives.","technical_approach":"Validity-Calibrated Reasoning Distillation (VCRD): at each prefix, both teacher and student propose a next-step continuation; an auxiliary judge scores their local validity under the same context; the relative validity then scales the distillation update magnitude. complexity，即训练样本中技能组合复杂度如何影响泛化到新组合。总体结果支持一个受限但清晰的结论：当模型原本已经具备相关电路时，新增软 token 可以作为技能选择器，而不是完整学习器。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25–35 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中对 skill-based continual learning 的 P1–P3 定义，以及
