skills. The proposed unit is not a task-specific prompt prefix, but a learned “skill word”: a new soft vocabulary item that can be placed in context to bias the model toward a particular capability and compose with other skills.","importance":"The main relevance for pretraining is the interface between frozen model capabilities, continual learning, and the tokenizer/embedding layer. Standard continual pretraining or fine-tuning updates weights and can introduce forgetting. In-context learning is limited by context and often lacks adaptation strength. Skill neologisms explore a narrower but useful regime: if the pretrained model already contains the circuits needed for a skill, can a small number of new embedding vectors select and recombine those circuits in a controlled way?","technical_approach":"The method has two design choices. First, skill-centered training uses data where every example requires the target skill while also involving diverse skills already mastered by the model. This discourages the token from becoming a dataset shortcut and pushes it toward a reusable procedural representation. Second, vocabulary-level integration adds learnable soft tokens as new vocabulary elements while freezing all model weights. This differs from conventional prompt tuning, which usually learns a task-bound prefix. Here the learned vectors are intended to behave like newly introduced words that can be inserted into different prompts. The formal setup follows a skill-composition view: examples are associated with tuples of skills, and the key test is whether a token trained with some combinations transfers to unseen combinations.","experiments":"The empirical setup is controlled rather than open-ended. The paper first shows that pretrained LLM vocabularies already contain tokens that appear to carry procedural biases. It then trains skill neologisms and evaluates three properties: learning without weight updates, composition with out-of-distribution skills, and zero-shot composition of independently learned skill tokens. The ablations study token capacity and the complexity of skill mixtures during training. A foundational challenge in this setting is exploration, which requires algorithms that enable the LLMs to generate informative comparisons that improve sample-efficiency in online RLHF. Existing exploration strategies often derive bonuses via on-policy expectations, which are difficult to estima","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-06T03:56:45+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_04477.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理 online RLHF 中的探索效率问题。关键矛盾是：模型训练时只能看到沿历史策略收集到的偏好比较，覆盖很不均匀；如果探索奖励依赖当前策略的 on-policy 期望，估计会很噪，模型可能过早压低一些尚未充分验证的响应区域。DEPO 想解决的是如何用历史偏好数据本身刻画覆盖不足，并把这种不确定性稳定地注入偏好优化。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接启发在 post-train 侧，尤其是 online alignment 和数据闭环。它的贡献不是提出新的语言建模目标，而是把 bandit/RL 中的 data-dependent UCB 思路迁移到 RLHF 数据采集：让模型不只优化当前偏好估计，也主动补齐表示空间中覆盖薄的方向。这个问题会影响 SFT/RL 数据效率，也会影响后续把在线反馈用于 continual post-training 的稳定性。","technical_approach":"方法叫 DEPO，即 Data-dependent Exploration for Preference Optimization。论文假设偏好满足 Bradley-Terry 模型，并在一个已知特征空间中表示单个回答；一对回答被拼成 pairwise feature。历史偏好比较会形成一个覆盖矩阵，类似线性 bandit 中的 design matrix。对新回答对，DEPO 根据该方向在历史数据中的覆盖程度计算 confidence radius：覆盖少的方向半径大，覆盖多的方向半径小。这个半径作为 UCB 探索 bonus，被加到偏好优化目标中，形成 reward bias。与依赖当前策略分布期望的探索方法相比，DEPO 的探索信号直接来自已收集数据的几何结构，因此更不容易因为当前策略短期收缩而丢掉未探索区域。论文还给出 data-dependent regret bound，界会随实际采样轨迹的表示几何变化，而不是只给最坏情形保证。","experiments":"论文声称在多个 online RLHF 基线上有稳定提升，并强调 DEPO 计算开销较小、支持在线更新。已给出的全文片段没有包含实验章节的具体表格，因此无法确认模型规模、prompt 数量、反馈轮数、胜率提升或消融数值。可确认的实验关注点应包括：与被动探索、on-policy bonus 探索、标准偏好优化方法的比较；探索 bonus 的消融；历史覆盖矩阵更新方式对性能和稳定性的影响。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 20 到 30 分钟。优先看方法定义中 pairwise feature、coverage matrix、confidence radius 和 reward bias 的公式，这决定了它是否能落到真实 LLM 训练里。其次看 regret bound 的依赖项，重点看它如何从 worst-case 复杂度变成 data-dependent 复杂度。实验部分如果可读到，应重点看三类图表：在线轮次上的 win rate 曲线、反馈样本效率曲线、bonus/覆盖度消融。实现层面需要关注 feature map 来自哪里；如果特征只是 reward model 或 LLM hidden state，表示漂移会是主要工程问题。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies exploration in online RLHF. The core issue is that preference data are collected along the training trajectory and have uneven coverage. Exploration bonuses based on on-policy expectations are hard to estimate from such limited historical data, so the policy may prematurely suppress response regions that are under-explored but potentially high value.","importance":"The main relevance is to the post-training side of the pretrain-posttrain loop. It does not introduce a new language modeling objective, but it gives a cleaner way to make online preference collection more sample-efficient. The useful idea is to measure uncertainty from the geometry of historical comparisons rather than from the current policy alone. This matters for online alignment, continual preference training, and any pipeline where the model’s own sampling distribution determines future supervision.","technical_approach":"DEPO, Data-dependent Exploration for Preference Optimization, builds a UCB-style exploration bonus from historical preference comparisons. The preference oracle is modeled with a Bradley-Terry form. Each response has a feature representation, and a response pair is represented by a concatenated pairwise feature. Historical comparisons define a coverage matrix over this feature space. For a new comparison, the method computes a confidence radius that is large along poorly covered directions and small along well-covered ones. This radius is injected into the preference optimization objective as a reward bias. Compared with exploration schemes based on on-policy expectations, the signal is tied to observed data coverage, so it is less sensitive to short-term policy collapse. The paper also provides a data-dependent regret bound that adapts to the representation geometry induced by the collected trajectory.","experiments":"The paper reports consistent gains over online RLHF baselines and positions DEPO as lightweight enough for online updates. The provided text does not include the experimental tables, so concrete numbers such as model size, feedback budget, win-rate gains, or ablation magnitudes cannot be verified here. However, existing unsupervised RL-based methods often lack the capacity to adapt to the model's evolving reasoning capabilities during training. Therefore, these methods can misdirect policy optimization in the absence of ground-truth supervision. To address this issue, we introduce FREIA, a novel RL-based algorithm built on two key innovations: (1) Free Ene","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-11T07:26:04+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_04065.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.ET","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"FREIA 处理的是无标注推理强化学习里的训练信号漂移问题。模型在训练早期、能力爬升期和收敛期需要不同的奖励偏好，但现有无监督方法常用固定规则：要么相信多数答案，要么相信单条轨迹的不确定性或置信度。这会在没有标准答案校验时放大错误共识，或奖励高置信但错误的少数路径。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在训练信号设计，而不是模型结构。它把无监督 self-improvement 拆成两个问题：奖励如何随模型能力变化，优势函数如何随 reward distribution 变化。这个视角对 RL pretrain、持续预训练后的自举式推理增强、以及无人工标注数据利用都有参考意义。贡献量级偏方法增量，但问题抓得准：无监督 RL 的主要风险不是没有 reward，而是 reward 在不同阶段含义不同。","technical_approach":"方法由两部分组成。第一是 Free Energy-Driven Reward，简称 FER。它借用自由能原则，把自我改进写成同时降低不确定性和探索新路径的过程。具体奖励不只看多数一致性，也保留少数候选的探索价值；训练早期避免被错误多数答案锁死，训练后期则逐步强化更稳定的共识。第二是 Adaptive Advantage Shaping，简称 AAS。它不把 reward 标准化当成固定操作，而是根据一批样本 reward 分布的偏度判断训练阶段。在 weak consensus 阶段，高 reward 样本稀少且未必可靠，AAS 会抑制异常高 reward 对 policy update 的过度牵引；在 strong consensus 阶段，偶发低 reward 样本不应被过度惩罚，AAS 会减轻这类负向梯度。与多数投票式方法相比，FREIA 不把 consensus 等同于 correctness；与 trajectory-intrinsic 方法相比，它也不把置信度或稀有性直接等同于好信号。","experiments":"实验覆盖九个 benchmark、三类推理任务，比较对象包括无监督 self-improvement 方法和基于共识或轨迹内在信号的 RL baseline。核心结果是 FREIA 在无外部标准答案监督的设置下整体优于这些无监督 baseline。引言中的案例显示，纯多数投票可能把错误多数答案给最高奖励，把正确少数答案给零奖励；纯自信息类信号则可能奖励罕见但错误的答案。消融重点应放在 FER 与 AAS 的单独贡献：FER 对错误共识有抑制作用，AAS 对早期 reward outlier 和后期偶发低 reward 的梯度放大有缓冲作用。具体提升幅度、每个任务的方差和显著性需要以实验表格为准。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，预计 20 到 30 分钟。优先看 Introduction 中 Figure 1 和 Figure 2，它们说明 reward 误导和 advantage 误导分别发生在哪里。Methodology 重点看 FER 的奖励分解，以及 AAS 如何用 reward skewness 判断阶段并重塑 advantage。实验部分建议只看主结果表、FER/AAS 消融、reward 分布或 entropy 曲线。如果关心 RL pretrain，可以额外检查它是否依赖固定采样数、固定 group size，以及在更大模型或更长训练下是否稳定。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"FREIA targets a nonstationary-signal problem in unsupervised RL for reasoning. Existing methods often use a fixed reward rule throughout training: population-based methods trust majority agreement, while trajectory-intrinsic methods trust uncertainty or confidence-like signals from individual reasoning paths. Without answer labels, both can push the policy in the wrong direction as the model’s capability changes.","importance":"The main value for pretraining research is the treatment of reward quality and training dynamics. The work separates unsupervised self-improvement into two coupled questions: how rewards should adapt as model capability evolves, and how advantage estimates should adapt as the reward distribution changes. This is relevant to RL-style continual pretraining, self-generated reasoning data, and label-free improvement loops. The method is not a new foundation model architecture, but it addresses a real failure mode in unsupervised RL: the same scalar reward can mean different things at different phases of training.","technical_approach":"FREIA has two components. Free Energy-Driven Reward, or FER, frames self-improvement as free-energy minimization and decomposes the signal into consensus alignment and exploration of alternative reasoning paths. The design avoids treating majority agreement as correctness, especially early in training when an incorrect answer may dominate the sampled population. Adaptive Advantage Shaping, or AAS, then changes the policy-gradient signal according to the shape of the reward distribution. It uses reward skewness as a phase indicator. In weak-consensus regimes, rare high-reward samples are not allowed to dominate updates. In strong-consensus regimes, occasional low-reward samples are not over-penalized. Compared with majority-vote rewards, FER keeps exploration alive; compared with intrinsic trajectory rewards, it does not equate rarity or self-confidence with correctness.","experiments":"The evaluation uses nine benchmarks across three reasoning categories and compares against unsupervised RL baselines based on consensus or trajectory-intrinsic signals. The reported pattern is that FREIA improves reasoning accuracy without external answer supervision. The motivating examples show two concrete errors: majority voting can assign maximum reward to a wrong majority answer and zero reward to a correct minority answer, while self-information-style rewards can favor rare but wrong answers. The most informative ablations are FER-only versus AAS-only, plus curves for reward distribution, entropy, and training stability. Exact per-benchmark gains should be read from the experimental tables rather than inferred from the introduction.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Start with Figures 1 and 2, since they state the two failure modes clearly: reward misdirection and static advantage shaping. Then read the methodology parts defining FER and AAS, especially the use of reward skewness as a phase signal. In the experiments, focus on the main benchmark table, the FER/AAS ablation, and any entropy or reward-distribution curves. Adaptive Power-Mean Policy Optimization for Improved LLM Reasoning","abstract":"Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is an essential paradigm that enhances the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing methods typically rely on static policy optimization schemes that misalign with the model's evolving reasoning capabilities. To address this issue, we propose Adaptive Power-Mean Policy Optimization (APMPO), which comprises two main innovations: Power-Mean Policy Optimization (PMPO) and Feedback-Adaptive Clipping (FAC). Specifi","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-11T07:34:59+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_04066.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.ET","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"APMPO 处理的是 RLVR 中 policy optimization 规则过于静态的问题。GRPO、DAPO 等算术平均目标容易放大少数高 reward 样本，带来过早收敛和 entropy collapse；GMPO 的几何平均更重视一致性，但在训练早期会压制尚不稳定的正确路径。固定 clipping 也有类似问题：reward 稳定时更新太保守，reward 波动时约束又不够强。","importance":"这篇更接近 post-train 算法改造，但对预训练研究仍有信息量。它强调 RL 训练中的目标函数和 trust region 不应固定，而应跟随 reward 统计特征变化。对 RLVR、reasoning RL、以及未来把 verifiable reward 放进 pretrain 或 mid-train 的方案来说，这个方向比单纯换 benchmark 更有参考价值。贡献主要是优化目标和 clipping 机制的自适应化，属于 GRPO 系列的实用算法改进。","technical_approach":"APMPO 包含两个模块。第一是 Power-Mean Policy Optimization，简称 PMPO。它用 power mean 统一算术平均和几何平均：算术平均对高 reward outlier 更敏感，适合发现稀有正信号；几何平均对低 reward 更敏感，适合要求多条推理路径一致。PMPO 通过调节 power 参数，让训练在信号放大和一致性约束之间移动，而不是固定采用某一种聚合方式。第二是 Feedback-Adaptive Clipping，简称 FAC。它根据当前 batch 的 reward 稳定性调整 clipping 区间。reward 更一致时，说明 policy improvement 方向较清楚，可以放宽 trust region；reward 波动较大时，说明反馈不稳定，需要收紧 clipping，避免一次更新把策略推向噪声方向。与 GRPO 的固定目标和固定 clip 相比，APMPO 的关键变化是把 reward statistics 作为优化器的反馈信号。","experiments":"实验覆盖九个 benchmark、三类推理任务，基线包括 GRPO、DAPO、GMPO 等 RLVR 方法。预分析部分比较了 GRPO 和 GMPO 的 training reward 与 policy entropy：GRPO 的算术平均能较早捕捉高 reward 样本，但伴随明显 entropy collapse；GMPO 缓解了路径级过拟合，但早期对不稳定正确路径不够敏感。主实验声称 APMPO 在整体推理准确率上优于这些基线。消融应重点看 PMPO 和 FAC 的单独作用：PMPO 是否真的改善 reward-entropy trade-off，FAC 是否降低训练波动并提升最终准确率。具体数值提升需要看主表和消融表。","reading_guide":"泛读 20 分钟左右即可。先看 Preliminary Analysis 的 Figure 1，那里给出了 GRPO 与 GMPO 的主要训练动态差异。Method 部分重点看 power mean 目标如何覆盖算术平均和几何平均，以及 power 参数如何自适应变化。FAC 部分只需确认 reward stability 的定义、clip bound 的更新形式和是否引入额外超参。实验部分建议看主结果、PMPO/FAC 消融、entropy 曲线和对 clipping 范围的敏感性分析。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"APMPO addresses the rigidity of policy optimization in RL with verifiable rewards. Arithmetic-mean objectives used by methods such as GRPO and DAPO can over-amplify rare high-reward samples, often causing early convergence and entropy collapse. Geometric-mean objectives such as GMPO enforce consistency but can suppress promising reasoning paths that are still unstable early in training. Fixed clipping has the same issue: it is too conservative when rewards are stable and too loose when feedback is noisy.","importance":"The work is mainly a post-training algorithmic refinement, but it is relevant to foundation-model training because it treats RL objectives as part of the training dynamics rather than as fixed recipes. For RLVR, reasoning RL, and possible RL-style mid-training, the useful idea is to let both the aggregation objective and the trust region respond to reward statistics. The contribution is best viewed as a practical GRPO-family improvement, not a change in model architecture or data scaling.","technical_approach":"APMPO combines two mechanisms. Power-Mean Policy Optimization, or PMPO, uses the generalized power mean to interpolate between arithmetic-mean and geometric-mean behavior. The arithmetic mean is sensitive to high-reward outliers and can help discover rare positive signals. The geometric mean is more sensitive to low rewards and encourages consistency across sampled reasoning paths. PMPO adapts the aggregation behavior instead of committing to either extreme. Feedback-Adaptive Clipping, or FAC, adjusts the clipping range using real-time reward stability. When rewards in a batch are consistent, the update direction is treated as more reliable and the trust region can be widened. When rewards fluctuate, the clipping range is tightened to reduce unstable policy movement. The central difference from GRPO is that reward statistics feed back into both the objective shape and the policy-update constraint.","experiments":"The evaluation spans nine benchmarks and three reasoning task families, with comparisons to RLVR baselines including GRPO, DAPO, and GMPO. The preliminary analysis shows the expected trade-off: GRPO finds high-reward samples early but suffers a sharp drop in policy entropy, while GMPO maintains better entropy but can be insufficiently responsive during early learning. The reported main results favor APMPO in overall reasoning accuracy. The key evidence to inspect is the decomposition between PMPO and FAC: whether PMPO improves the reward-versus-entropy trade-off, and whether FAC reduces update instability while preserving final accuracy. Exact gains should be taken from the main and ablation tables.","reading_guide":"Skim in roughly 20 minutes. Read the preliminary analysis first, especially the figure comparing GRPO and GMPO reward and entropy curves. Then inspect the method section for the power-mean objective, the adaptation rule for the power parameter, and the definition of reward stability used by FAC. In the experiments, focus on the main result table, PMPO/FAC ablations, entropy curves, and sensitivity to clipping bounds or reward-statistic thresholds.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读 PMPO 目标和 FAC clipping 公式即可；若实验没有同算力 PPO/GRPO ablation，结论只能当 objective-shaping 线索。","en":"Read the PMPO objective and FAC clipping equations; without compute-matched PPO/GRPO ablations, the result is mainly a clue about objective shaping."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Harbin Institute of Technology (Shenzhen)","Peng Cheng Laboratory","The Chinese University of Hong Kong"],"authors":["Yiming Huang","Zhenbo Shi","Shuzheng Gao","Cuiyun Gao","Peiyi Han","Chuanyi Liu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["rlvr","policy-optimization","reasoning","adaptive-clipping","post-training"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04066","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.04066","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.04468","title":"Stabilizing LLM Supervised Fine-Tuning via Explicit Distributional Control","abstract":"Post-training large language models (LLMs) often suffers from catastrophic forgetting, where improvements on a target objective degrade previously acquired capabilities. Recent evidence suggests that this phenomenon is primarily driven by excessive distributional drift during optimization. Motivated by this perspective, we propose Anchored Learning, a simple framework that explicitly controls distributional updates during offline fine-tuning via a dynamically evolving moving anchor. Instead of m","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-06T03:48:15+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_04468.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是离线 SFT 中一个很具体但长期存在的问题：当目标数据分布和基座模型已有能力分布差异较大时，标准最大似然更新会让模型在分布空间里走得过远，结果是目标任务上升了，但原有能力明显退化。作者不是把遗忘归因于某个单一模块，而是把问题收束为“每一步更新的分布漂移缺少显式控制”。在这个视角下，固定 reference 的 KL 正则、固定 teacher 蒸馏、以及常规 trust-region 近似都不够贴合真实优化轨迹，因为它们约束的是一个静态目标，而不是当前模型附近的局部移动。","importance":"对预训练/后训练接口的意义在于，它把“RL 比 SFT 更不容易忘”这类经验现象，转成了一个可离线实现的分布控制机制。贡献不在于提出更强的任务学习器，而在于给出一种简单、低工程复杂度的稳定化方案：不需要 on-policy 采样，不需要 replay，不需要参数隔离，也不依赖已知预训练数据。对基座研究员更有价值的点有两个。第一，它把灾难性遗忘和 KL 轨迹控制直接绑定，适合作为 mid-train、continual pretrain、domain adaptation 的通用分析框架。第二，它说明“参考分布是否随训练共同演化”比“是否加 KL”更关键，这对后续设计 annealed SFT、offline RL-style post-training、甚至 pretrain cooldown 阶段的保守更新都有启发。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Anchored Learning。核心做法不是直接让当前模型去拟合固定 base/reference，也不是只做任务 NLL，而是在每次迭代构造一个“移动锚点”分布，再让模型朝这个中间目标蒸馏。这个锚点由当前模型分布和冻结参考模型分布插值得到，因此目标不是全局静态的，而是沿着训练轨迹不断更新。\n\n从机制上看，它把一次可能很激进的全局迁移，拆成一串局部、保守的分布更新。作者给了两种实现：概率空间 anchoring 和 logit 空间 anchoring。前者直接在输出分布上插值，后者在 logits 上插值后再 softmax。两者都能导出每步 KL 上界，但 logit-space 版本在实现上更接近现有训练栈，也更容易和 teacher distillation 兼容。\n\n训练目标可以理解成“任务监督 + 向锚点蒸馏”的组合，但和普通蒸馏不同，teacher 不是固定的外部模型，而是 current model 与 frozen reference 的混合体。这样做的原因很明确：如果只盯住固定 reference，当目标任务和 reference 差异大时，任务梯度与 KL 梯度会长期冲突，优化会变成拉扯；如果完全不约束，又会快速偏离原分布。移动锚点相当于在两者之间搭一条连续路径，让模型每一步只走一小段。\n\n理论部分的重点不是泛泛证明收敛，而是证明每次 anchored update 对当前分布的 KL 漂移有线性上界。这个结论给方法提供了 trust-region 式解释：虽然没有显式求解 constrained optimization，但通过构造中间目标，实际效果等价于把更新限制在局部邻域内。作者也讨论了为什么这种局部控制比静态 KL 更贴合分布几何：静态 reference 约束的是“离起点多远”，而 anchored learning 约束的是“下一步离当前位置多远”。后者更符合非凸训练中的稳定性需求。","experiments":"实验主要围绕 gain–stability trade-off 展开，而不是只看目标任务分数。评测集包括 iGSM、MedCalc、IFEval，分别覆盖数学、医学计算和指令遵循。论文最醒目的结果是：标准 SFT 在 iGSM 和 MedCalc 上会带来超过 53% 的能力退化，而 Anchored Learning 能把退化压到 5% 以下，同时仍保持接近最优的任务增益；文中给出的例子是 iGSM 上达到 75.2% 的增益。\n\n作者强调 Pareto frontier 结果：在相同或相近目标收益下，Anchored Learning 的遗忘更小；若要求相近稳定性，Anchored Learning 的任务提升又高于固定 KL、固定蒸馏等基线。这说明它不是单纯把学习率调小换稳定，而是在收益和保留能力之间找到更好的折中。\n\n消融部分值得看三类。第一，anchor 形式的比较：moving anchor 明显优于 frozen teacher / static KL，说明“动态参考”是主要增益来源。第二，插值强度或锚点系数的变化：系数过小会退化成普通 SFT，过大则会过度保守，任务收益下降，存在较宽但非无限宽的有效区间。第三，概率空间与 logit 空间实现：两者都有效，但在不同任务上稳定性和优化难度略有差异，提示该方法并不是某个特定参数化技巧偶然奏效。\n\n从全文信息看，实验更偏机制验证而不是大规模 frontier model 训练。没有看到超大参数量或超长训练 token 的展示，因此现阶段更适合把它视为一种后训练稳定化原语，而不是已经在超大规模上完全验证的工业配方。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看 Introduction 和表 1，先建立作者要替代什么：不是 replay，不是 RL，不是参数隔离，而是纯离线条件下的分布控制。然后重点看方法定义部分，弄清 anchor 到底是如何由当前模型和冻结 reference 构造出来的，尤其区分 probability-space 和 logit-space 两种版本。\n\n如果只花 20–30 分钟，建议抓三处：\n1. 理论命题里每步 KL 上界的形式，理解它为什么能支持“局部 trust-region”解释；\n2. 主结果图里 gain–stability frontier 的位置关系，确认它是否真的优于 static KL / distillation，而不是只在单点上占优；\n3. anchor 系数消融，判断方法是否需要精细调参。\n\n更值得深挖的点有两个。一个是它和 RLHF / GRPO 一类 on-policy 更新的关系：论文把 RL 的稳定性解释为隐式局部更新，这个桥接很有启发。另一个是它能否前移到 continual pretrain 或 domain-adaptive pretraining：如果把 frozen reference 换成 pretrain checkpoint，把目标数据换成新域语料，这套框架可能直接变成一种保守继续预训练方法。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a precise failure mode in offline supervised fine-tuning: when the target data distribution is far from the model’s existing behavior, standard NLL optimization moves the model too aggressively in distribution space, improving the target objective while eroding previously acquired capabilities. The authors frame catastrophic forgetting not as an isolated optimization bug, but as a consequence of insufficient control over per-step distributional drift. Under this view, static KL regularization, fixed-teacher distillation, and global trust-region style constraints are all misaligned with the actual local geometry of the optimization trajectory.","importance":"Its value for pretrain and post-train research is that it turns the empirical observation—on-policy RL tends to forget less than offline SFT—into a simple offline mechanism for distribution control. The contribution is not a stronger task learner, but a low-complexity stabilization primitive: no replay, no parameter isolation, no on-policy rollouts, and no access to pretraining data. Two aspects matter for foundation-model researchers. First, it makes KL trajectory control a central lens for studying forgetting in continual adaptation, domain tuning, and cooldown-style training. Second, it suggests that whether the reference distribution co-evolves with training may matter more than merely adding a KL term. In this paper, we uncover a critical mechanism underlying safety degradation by analyzing parameter dynamics, where benign f","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-06T07:17:33+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_04572.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作关注一个很具体的安全退化问题：模型已经做过安全对齐，但在少量看似无害的数据上继续微调后，安全行为会明显变弱。它要回答的不是“有害样本如何攻击模型”，而是“无害样本为什么也会推动模型远离安全状态，以及能否在样本级别提前打分”。","importance":"对预训练和后训练接口有一定启发。它把安全退化从静态的参数差异，推进到训练过程中的参数轨迹分析。这个视角有助于理解 SFT、LoRA、持续微调为什么会破坏先前对齐出的行为边界。贡献量级不是新的基座训练范式，但对安全数据过滤、持续预训练数据审计、企业侧适配前的风险评估有实用意义。尤其是它强调“良性语料也有方向性风险”，这比只做毒性分类更接近真实微调场景。","technical_approach":"核心方法是 SQSD，Sample-Level Quantification of Safety Degradation。作者先构造两个参数空间方向：安全方向和危险方向。做法类似 task vector：从同一个基座模型出发，分别得到一个安全对齐模型和一个有害倾向模型，再用参数位移表示两个语义方向。安全方向来自在 PKU-SafeRLHF-10K 上做 DPO 后的模型位移；危险方向来自在 Aegis、BeaverTails 等数据上做有害倾向 SFT 后的模型位移。随后，微调过程中每个样本诱导的参数更新会被投影到这两个方向上。若一个样本的更新更接近危险方向、同时远离安全方向，它的安全退化风险就更高。这个设计比只看梯度范数或参数扰动大小更细，因为同样大小的更新，方向不同，对安全行为的影响可能完全不同。作者还用一阶 Taylor 近似把参数更新方向和安全/不安全模型的偏好差异联系起来，使 SQSD 不只是经验打分，而是和模型输出行为有可解释关系。","experiments":"实验覆盖三个模型和两个数据集，并比较了 LoRA 与全参数微调场景。文中强调一个关键现象：即使只用约 100 条良性样本微调，也可能显著削弱安全行为。参数轨迹分析显示，微调不是一次性跳到不安全区域，而是在训练过程中逐步向危险方向累积漂移。SQSD 的样本级风险分数能够区分不同良性样本对这种漂移的贡献，并且在模型架构、参数规模和微调方式之间有一定迁移性。相比只选极端高风险样本的方法，连续风险打分避免了把问题退化成触发模式识别，对中等风险样本更友好。由于提供内容中没有完整表格，具体指标数值不宜过度展开。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 Introduction 和 Safety Degradation in Parameter Dynamics，重点看安全方向、危险方向如何构造，以及样本更新如何投影到两个方向上。公式部分值得看清楚：LoRA 的有效权重漂移如何定义，task vector 如何被用于安全语义锚点。实验部分优先看三类结果：参数轨迹是否真的向危险方向累积漂移；SQSD 与实际安全退化的相关性；LoRA 到全参数微调、不同模型之间的迁移性。预计阅读 25 到 35 分钟。若关心持续预训练数据审计，可以重点深挖风险分数是否能和数据选择、loss reweighting 或安全保持正则结合。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work studies a narrow but important failure mode: a safety-aligned LLM can lose safety behavior after fine-tuning on a small set of benign samples. The question is not how explicitly harmful data attacks a model, but why apparently safe data can still push parameters toward unsafe behavior, and whether this risk can be scored at the individual-sample level.","importance":"The main value is in the interface between alignment and continued fine-tuning. The paper moves the analysis from static before-versus-after parameter differences to the trajectory of parameter updates during training. This is useful for understanding why SFT, LoRA adaptation, and continual fine-tuning can erode previously learned safety constraints. It is not a new foundation-model training paradigm, but it gives a concrete tool for data auditing before downstream adaptation. A key lesson is that benign-looking data can still have directional risk in parameter space, which standard toxicity filters will miss.","technical_approach":"$1e","experiments":"The experiments cover three models and two datasets, with both LoRA and full fine-tuning settings. One motivating observation is that roughly 100 benign fine-tuning samples can already cause clear safety degradation. The parameter-trajectory analysis suggests that degradation is gradual: updates accumulate in a direction aligned with unsafe behavior rather than appearing only as a final-state difference. SQSD separates benign samples by their contribution to this directional drift and shows transfer across model architectures, model scales, and fine-tuning regimes. This approach often suffers from training instability and","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-06T15:31:50+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_05040.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是 on-policy self-distillation 中单纯做 KL matching 的不稳定问题。现有方法把同一个模型在增强上下文下的分布当作 teacher，让 student 去匹配它；但这个 teacher 并不真正更强，且 KL 训练容易压缩推理轨迹中的不确定性表达、自检和纠错，长期训练可能损害推理能力。","importance":"这篇工作属于 post-training，但对预训练研究有间接价值。它强调训练信号质量比“更密的 token-level 信号”更关键：如果 teacher 只是同参模型加上下文，KL 目标可能把偏差固化。论文把 self-distillation 改写成 reward-regularized policy improvement，给出了一个更合理的目标分布：不是原样复制 teacher，而是在 teacher 分布附近向高奖励样本移动。这对 RL pretrain、长链推理训练和自举式数据合成都有参考意义。","technical_approach":"方法先从目标函数上改造自蒸馏。传统做法最小化 student 与 context-augmented teacher 的 KL；PBSD 在 teacher anchoring 的同时加入 reward maximization。该目标的解析最优解不是 teacher 分布本身，而是按潜在 reward 对 teacher 分布重加权后的策略，形式上接近 reward-weighted teacher policy。基于这个结论，训练不再只做 token-level KL，而是构造 teacher sample 与 student on-policy sample 之间的 preference gap，用偏好学习推动 student 增加高奖励输出的概率，同时保留 teacher 提供的结构性约束。论文还给出统计分析，把 self-distillation 转化为诱导的 preference-learning 问题，讨论在什么条件下相关的上下文 teacher 会比外部 teacher 带来更小的估计误差。","experiments":"实验覆盖数学推理和工具使用，并在多个模型规模上比较 PBSD 与 KL 型 self-distillation、on-policy distillation 和 RL 类基线。论文报告 PBSD 在总体指标上更稳，训练过程中推理性能退化更少，同时保留 self-distillation 的 token 效率优势。值得注意的实验现象是，KL matching 会倾向于产生更短、更自信的推理轨迹，但这不一定对应更高正确率；PBSD 通过偏好差距和 reward 正则缓解了这种过度压缩。具体参数量、数据规模和提升幅度在给定内容中没有足够信息可核验。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 30–40 分钟。优先看 Method 中从 KL self-distillation 到 reward-regularized objective 的推导，尤其是解析最优策略为什么是 reward-reweighted teacher，而不是 teacher 本身。随后看 preference gap 的构造和 on-policy sampling 细节。实验部分重点看训练曲线、推理长度变化、数学推理与工具使用的分项结果。若只关心预训练接口，可重点读目标函数和稳定性分析。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a failure mode in on-policy self-distillation. Existing methods treat the same model under an augmented prompt as the teacher and train the student distribution to match it with KL. This can be unstable: the model may shorten reasoning traces, suppress uncertainty and self-correction, and lose reasoning performance over training.","importance":"The work is primarily post-training, but it is relevant to pretraining-oriented research on training signals and self-improvement loops. It shows that dense token-level supervision is not automatically a better signal. If the teacher is only a context-augmented version of the same model, KL matching can preserve or amplify its biases. The proposed reward-regularized view is a useful template for RL pretraining, reasoning-oriented continual training, and self-generated data pipelines: stay close to a useful anchor, but move probability mass toward higher-reward outputs.","technical_approach":"PBSD starts by replacing pure KL matching with a teacher-anchored, reward-regularized objective. The analytic optimum is not the teacher distribution itself; it is a reward-reweighted version of the teacher policy. This motivates a preference-based training procedure. The student still samples on-policy, but optimization is driven by preference gaps between teacher and student samples rather than by blindly matching token probabilities. The method therefore keeps the structural signal provided by the contextual teacher while allowing policy improvement under a latent reward. The paper also analyzes the induced preference-learning problem statistically and gives conditions under which a relevant contextual teacher can be preferable to an external teacher in terms of estimation error.","experiments":"The evaluation covers mathematical reasoning and tool use across multiple model scales. The reported pattern is that PBSD improves training stability over KL-based self-distillation baselines and keeps the token-efficiency advantage of self-distillation. A key diagnostic is reasoning behavior: KL matching tends to make outputs shorter and more overconfident, which does not reliably improve correctness. PBSD mitigates this by using reward-aware preference gaps. 理论谱系与信息论式约束：借用社会科学中的 DeGroot 共识动力学与 Banerjee 信息级联，强调在只交换内部信号、共享参数/归纳偏置的系统里，迭代会更强地收敛到初始意见的加权组合，而不是外部真值；这给出一种可证伪的机制解释：闭环迭代更像在做“表述空间的再参数化与一致化”，而不是在证据空间里增加信息量。\n3) 诊断指标与对照设计：指出既有 MAD se, but it places a hard constraint on a common pipeline that feeds closed-loop deliberation traces into distillation/RL/SFT as “better reasoning data”. bli","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-06T15:25:37+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_05029.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的是一个比“预测做得好是否就学到了正确状态表示”更尖锐的问题：当训练目标只是最小化未来预测误差时，学到的表示是否会优先编码真正的系统因果变量，而不是更容易预测、但对因果解释无益的环境变量。全文给出的答案是否定的，而且不是优化没做好，而是目标函数本身就有结构性偏置。在线性高斯动力系统里，只要环境模态更慢、更稳定、噪声更小，最优预测表示就会偏向环境而非系统，出现作者称为 predictive-causal gap 的现象。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义不在于它直接给出一个可用的新算法，而在于它明确指出了“纯预测目标”的边界。很多自监督预训练、世界模型、表征学习方法默认把 next-step prediction 或 latent prediction 当作通用代理目标，隐含假设是：预测得越好，表示越接近可迁移、可解释、可控制的潜在状态。全文证明这个假设在相当干净的设定下都不成立。对 LLM 和更广义 foundation model 而言，这个结论提醒两点：第一，压低训练 loss 不等于学到任务所需的因果结构；第二，当数据里存在更稳定、更低熵、但并非目标机制本身的统计线索时，模型会系统性地吸附到这些“容易预测的慢变量”上。这对预训练目标设计、数据构造、世界模型表征、以及 post-train 阶段为什么需要额外监督或交互信号，都有直接启发。","technical_approach":"方法上分两层。第一层是严格的构造性定理。作者从二维线性高斯系统出发，把状态分成 system 变量 s 和 environment 变量 e，考虑一维线性编码器 y_t = w^T x_t，并配最优线性一步预测器。核心分析对象是稳态下的最小 latent self-prediction risk。作者先写出平稳协方差满足的离散 Lyapunov 方程，再在上三角动力系统下给出闭式解，随后比较三类编码器：只看 system 的 NZ projector、只看 environment 的编码器、以及任意角度的混合编码器。结论是，NZ projector 不仅可能输给纯 environment 编码器，还可能输给一个内部角度的混合最优解。补充材料里给了显式参数例子：a_s=0.05, a_e=0.98, c=-0.90, q_s=0.05, q_e=0.10 时，R(w_NZ)≈0.174，R(w_env)=0.100，而全局最优角度在 43.7° 左右，风险约 0.074。也就是说，最优预测表示既不是纯系统，也不是纯环境，而是朝着更可预测的方向倾斜。\n\n第二层是大规模神经实验验证。作者并不满足于线性定理，而是系统扫描 2695 个神经网络配置，在更高维线性高斯动力学上训练预测模型，统计 encoder sensitivity 分配到 system 自由度上的比例，定义为 causal fidelity。结果显示平均 causal fidelity 只有 0.49，超过 0.70 的配置仅 2.5%。随着维度增加，问题急剧恶化；在 N=100 时，最优编码器对 system 几乎失明，fidelity 约为 1e-8，但预测误差却比“因果表示”低 92%。这一步很关键，因为它把“存在反例”推进成“在大范围配置下普遍发生的训练偏置”。\n\n从机制上看，作者的论证很清楚：预测目标奖励的是可压缩的时间稳定性，而不是因果相关性。慢变量、低噪声变量、强自相关变量，会在一步预测里贡献更高的可预测性，因此编码器会把有限表示容量优先分给它们。若 system 变量更快、更噪、或被 environment 驱动，单纯预测就没有理由恢复 system 本身。","experiments":"关键实验分成理论例证和神经网络扫描两部分。\n\n1. 理论构造例子：在二维系统中，作者给出闭式可算的反例。对参数 a_s=0.05, c=-0.90, a_e=0.98, q_s=0.05, q_e=0.10，NZ 编码器的最小预测风险约 0.174，纯环境编码器为 0.100，而最优混合编码器达到 0.074，最优方向约 43.7°。这说明“只编码系统变量”在预测目标下严格次优，而且次优不是边界现象，最优点稳定地落在内部角度。\n\n2. 高维神经实验：作者训练了 2695 个神经网络配置来做预测表示学习，覆盖不同维度、噪声和动力学设定。总体平均 causal fidelity 为 0.49，只有 2.5% 的配置超过 0.70，说明大多数模型并没有把主要敏感度放在 system 变量上。\n\n3. 维度效应：随着状态维度增大，predictive-causal gap 明显放大。在 N=100 时，最优编码器的 causal fidelity 下降到约 10^-8，几乎完全不看 system；与此同时，它的预测误差比因果表示低 92%。这组数字很有说服力，因为它说明错误表示不是“性能差不多但解释不同”，而是预测目标会主动偏好错误但更易预测的表示。\n\n4. 鲁棒性：补充证明里用隐函数定理说明内部最优角度对参数扰动是稳定的，不是某个精细调参点才成立。也就是说，这个 gap 不是 measure-zero 异常，而是一个开放邻域内都存在的结构现象。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合带着问题读，而不是顺着摘要读完就结束。\n\n建议先看主文里对 predictive-causal gap 的定义，以及 causal fidelity 的度量方式，先把作者到底在比较什么弄清楚。随后直接看定理陈述和二维反例，不必一开始就陷进全部证明细节。真正值得精读的是补充材料 S1：\n1. 看离散 Lyapunov 方程和上三角系统的闭式协方差解，理解风险为什么能写成显式函数；\n2. 看 Proposition / Lemma 中 NZ、environment、interior optimum 三者的比较，尤其是为什么最优解会落在内部角度；\n3. 看高维嵌入论证，理解这个现象如何从 2D 反例推广到更高维。\n\n如果时间有限，优先抓三件事：一是 impossibility theorem 的逻辑结构；二是 2695 个配置的神经实验统计；三是维度升高后 fidelity 崩塌而 prediction error 继续改善这一组结果。阅读时间大约 30–45 分钟可以抓住主结论；若要把证明吃透，可能需要 1–2 小时。\n\n值得深挖的点有两个。第一，作者分析的是一步 latent self-prediction，这和语言模型的 next-token 目标并不等价，迁移到 LLM 时要注意目标结构差异。第二，论文给出的是“不可能性”而不是“修复方案”，因此更像一个警示：如果目标里没有显式区分因果变量与环境慢变量的机制，单靠预测损失很难自动学出来。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether predictive representation learning can recover the causal state of a system, rather than merely whatever is easiest to predict. The answer, under a clean linear-Gaussian setting, is no. When the objective is only to minimize future prediction error, the optimal encoder can systematically track environmental modes instead of the system variables one actually wants. The paper calls this mismatch the predictive-causal gap, and argues that it is a property of the objective itself rather than an optimization failure.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is mostly diagnostic, not algorithmic. Many self-supervised and predictive objectives rely on an implicit hope: if a representation predicts well, it should also capture the right latent structure. This paper shows that the implication can fail even in a simple, analyzable regime. For foundation-model researchers, the message is concrete: lower predictive loss does not guarantee recovery of task-relevant or causally meaningful structure. If the data contain slower, cleaner, more persistent nuisance factors, a predictive objective may allocate representation capacity to those factors first. That has implications for world models, latent-state learning, multimodal pretraining, and for why post-training or interaction-derived signals may be needed to correct what pure prediction leaves unresolved.","technical_approach":"$20","experiments":"$21","reading_guide":"$22","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读定理条件和 N=100 结果即可；它不能直接外推到 LLM，但会削弱“预测即因果”的默认乐观。","en":"Read the theorem conditions and the N=100 result; it does not transfer directly to LMs, but it weakens the casual “prediction learns causality” prior."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Soochow University","State Key Laboratory of Bioinspired Interface Material Science","Institute of Nano & Functional Materials, Soochow University"],"authors":["Kejun Liu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["predictive-learning","causal-representation","representation-learning","training-objectives","mechanistic-analysis"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05029","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.05029","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.04764","title":"Elicitation Matters: How Prompts and Query Protocols Shape LLM Surrogates under Sparse Observations","abstract":"Large language models are increasingly used as surrogate models for low-data optimization, but their optimizer-facing prediction and its uncertainty remain poorly understood. LLM 做 surrogate 更准，而是一个更基础的问题：在稀疏观测下，优化器真正从 LLM 那里“拿到”的 surrogate belief 到底是什么，以及这个 belief 会如何被 prompt 文本和 query protocol 改写。作者指出，在低数据、目标函数仍高度欠定的场景里，只看点预测误差或 calibration 不够，因为真正影响 Bayesian optimization 或实验设计的是：模型的不确定性是否跟“仍有多少与样本一致的函数可能性”对齐，以及新增证据后这种不确定性是否按合理方式更新。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接关系不算强，但对 ICL、模型先验、语言接口如何塑造行为分布这几个问题有启发。它的重要性不在于提出一个更好的 surrogate，而在于把“prompt 是有效先验”“query protocol 改变外显信念”“顺序证据更新并不稳定”这些常被经验性提及的现象，做成了较系统的行为分析框架。对基座研究员来说，这篇工作提醒了一点：当我们把 LLM 当作无梯度学习器或黑盒贝叶斯器件时，输出的不只是函数值，还包括一个由预训练偏置、提示词和解码协议共同决定的操作性 posterior。这个 posterior 未必和样本约束下的真实歧义结构一致。","technical_approach":"作者先把研究对象定义得很清楚：不是模型内部真实信念，而是 optimizer-facing、protocol-conditioned surrogate belief，也就是在固定观测集 D、prompt p 和 query protocol π 下，LLM 对查询点值向量给出的预测分布。这个定义很重要，因为它把问题从“模型心里怎么想”转成“优化器实际能观察到什么”。\n\n在此基础上，论文比较了两类 query protocol。POINTWISE 是对每个查询点独立提问、独立回答；JOINT 是把多个查询点一次性放进同一个 autoregressive completion。两者即使在边际方差上相近，也可能因为跨位置相关结构不同，而给出完全不同的曲线级不确定性。作者还系统改变 prompt 的三个维度：提示风格（中性、强调欠定性、无关警告）、结构信息强度（无结构、函数族、结构提示、参数提示）、以及故意错误的结构信息。这里的核心判断是：prompt 不只是表面措辞，而是行为上的 effective prior，会系统性地推动 surrogate belief 向某类函数解释偏移。\n\n方法上的一个亮点是提出 uncertainty-alignment criterion。它不是简单看预测置信度和真实误差是否匹配，而是拿模型侧不确定性去对照“样本一致函数集合”仍然保留的歧义轮廓。也就是说，在 underdetermined setting 里，好的 surrogate 不只是误差小，而是该不确定的地方真的不确定。论文同时区分了 token-level confidence proxy 和 output-level uncertainty：前者用数值答案 token 的 NLL 构造，后者通过固定条件下重复采样得到输出分布。这个区分有助于说明，语言模型的 token 置信度未必等价于函数层面的 epistemic uncertainty。\n\n另一个值得注意的设计是顺序证据实验。作者不是只比较最终给定全部观测后的结果，而是按不同顺序逐步加入观测，观察 surrogate belief 如何更新。结果显示其置信度更新可能出现非单调的 dip-and-recovery，且 valley 的位置受证据顺序影响。这说明 LLM 的 ICL 更新并不满足经典贝叶斯后验那种稳定、顺序无关的性质，至少在外显行为层面如此。","experiments":"实验覆盖了受控函数推断任务和 Bayesian optimization 场景。函数族包括 Gaussian、quadratic、linear、sinusoidal、logistic，目的是保持可解释和可控，而不是追求任务复杂度。模型方面覆盖 Llama、GPT、Qwen 等不同家族，也包括开源和闭源、单模态和多模态模型。作者刻意不启用 CoT，而是测“直觉式”数值判断，这样更接近把 LLM 当 surrogate 使用时的默认接口。\n\n主要结果有四类。第一，结构性 prompt 确实像先验：给对的结构提示时，预测和不确定性更接近真实函数族；给错的结构提示时，模型会被系统性带偏，而且这种偏移会进入下游 acquisition decision，最终影响 regret。第二，POINTWISE 与 JOINT 会诱导不同的 surrogate belief。论文的结论是 POINTWISE 更敏感于歧义、也更忠于观测约束；JOINT 更连贯，但更容易牺牲对局部样本约束的忠实性。第三，在顺序证据设置下，模型置信度不是单调收缩，而会出现类似 Dunning–Kruger 的先降后升曲线，且不同证据顺序会改变低谷位置。第四，仅用 realized error 或常规 calibration 指标不足以评价 surrogate，因为在样本仍欠定时，模型可能预测得不差，但不确定性完全没有跟剩余歧义对齐。\n\n从给出的全文片段看，摘要没有列出统一的单个 headline 数字，论文更强调现象稳定性和决策后果，而不是某个 benchmark 上的绝对提升。泛读时应重点看 uncertainty-alignment 指标的定义、不同 prompt/protocol 下 belief divergence 的量化，以及 BO 实验里 acquisition 与 regret 的变化。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合带着问题读，而不是按 benchmark 结果读。先看 Introduction 和 Problem Setting，弄清作者定义的 protocol-conditioned surrogate belief 是什么，以及为什么 calibration 在 underdetermined regime 里不够。然后看 uncertainty-alignment criterion 的形式化定义，这是整篇最有方法含量的部分。接着看 POINTWISE vs JOINT 的实验图，以及 sequential evidence 的置信度曲线，这两部分最能说明“语言接口改变 surrogate 行为”不是一句泛泛而谈的话。\n\n如果只打算泛读，建议顺序是：问题定义 → prompt/query protocol 设计 → uncertainty 指标 → 受控函数实验 → BO 实验。预计 25–35 分钟能抓住主要结论。值得深挖的点有三个：一是 sample-consistent ambiguity 的参考分布 q_D 如何构造，是否会影响结论稳健性；二是 token-level confidence 与 output-level uncertainty 的偏差在不同模型家族上是否一致；三是这些现象究竟来自预训练先验、数值输出格式限制，还是 autoregressive 解码带来的序列耦合。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not mainly about making LLM surrogates more accurate. It asks a more basic question: under sparse observations, what surrogate belief does an optimizer actually receive from an LLM, and how is that belief changed by prompt wording and query protocol? In low-data regimes, many functions remain consistent with the observed samples, so point prediction error and standard calibration are not enough. What matters is whether the model’s uncertainty tracks the residual ambiguity left by the data, and whether that uncertainty updates sensibly as new evidence arrives.","importance":"Its direct connection to pretraining is limited, but it is relevant to in-context learning, implicit priors from pretraining, and the way language interfaces shape model behavior. The contribution is not a better surrogate method; it is a cleaner behavioral framework for studying three recurring but often loosely stated phenomena: prompts act as effective priors, query protocols change the exposed belief, and sequential evidence updates are not stable in a Bayesian sense. For foundation-model work, the useful reminder is that when an LLM is used as a gradient-free learner or a black-box surrogate, the optimizer does not receive a neutral posterior. It receives an operational predictive object shaped jointly by pretraining regularities, prompt text, and decoding protocol.","technical_approach":"$23","experiments":"$24","reading_guide":"$25","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇不改 pretrain，但会改你看待“模型不确定性”的方式；重点读 uncertainty-alignment 定义和顺序敏感实验，别把 elicitation 当纯包装。","en":"This will not change pretraining directly, but it should change how you think about model uncertainty; read the uncertainty-alignment setup and the order-sensitivity experiments, not just the headline."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Imperial College London","Dyson School of Design Engineering","Imperial College"],"authors":["Ge Lei","Samuel J. The method uses attention matrices to estimate uncertainty without requiring repeated sampling or external models. Specifically, we measure the Kullback-Leibler divergence between each attention head's distribution and a uniform reference distribution, and use these features in a logistic regression probe. 当作离散概率分布，并计算其相对均匀分布 U 的 KL 散度：KL(A || U)。直觉是：均匀注意力对应“模型不知道该看哪里”（更不确定），而更集中的注意力对应更强的内部确定性。\n实现上：\n- 在单次生成过程中提取各层各头的 KL(A||U) 特征；\n- 将这些特征聚合后，用一个带 lasso 正则的 logistic regression probe 预测答案是否正确。probe LLM generations that does not rely on repeated sampling or external verifier models, and that goes beyond output-space likelihood signals which often correlate with fluency rather than factual correctness.","importance":"This is primarily a reliability/interpretability signal paper rather than a new pretraining recipe. Its value to foundation-model training is the empirical finding that attention-shape statistics—specifically divergence from uniform—carry predictive information about correctness, concentrated in mid layers and around factual tokens (entities, numbers). Potential downstream uses include training diagnostics, data filtering/weighting during post-training or continual pretraining, and possibly auxiliary calibration regularizers (not established here).","technical_approach":"At each generation step, treat each attention head’s attention weights as a discrete distribution over the context positions. Compute KL divergence to a uniform reference distribution U: KL(A || U). The intuition is that near-uniform attention reflects uncertainty about where to attend, while concentrated attention reflects stronger internal certainty.\nFeatures are collected across layers/heads in a single forward pass and fed into a lasso-regularized logistic regression probe to predict whether the produced answer is correct. The probe is meant to be a lightweight aggregator over heads/layers rather than a heavy second-stage model. The paper also analyzes where the signal lives (layerwise) and which token types drive it (named entities, numbers).","experiments":"The provided excerpt does not include datasets, metrics, or numeric results, so concrete AUROC/accuracy numbers and baseline gaps cannot be quoted. orientation","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-06T13:25:13+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_04893.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文讨论的不是如何提升模型能力，而是一个更基础的诊断边界问题：如果把 self-attention 看成 query 到 key 的双部传输算子，那么现有很多基于谱量的 attention 诊断，到底能看见什么、看不见什么。作者指出，常见谱诊断往往只能看到“容量”或“混合程度”，却看不到“方向性”。这会导致两类机制上相反的失败模式——注意力过度集中形成瓶颈，和注意力过度扩散导致信号稀释——在某些对称谱统计下表现得很像。论文要解决的核心问题，就是给这种不可分辨性建立一个严格的结构性上界。","importance":"对 LLM 基座研究的直接相关性不如训练方法论文强，但对机制分析和训练诊断有方法论价值。它的贡献不是提出一个更好的 hallucination detector，而是澄清一类诊断工具的能力边界：凡是只依赖奇异值或对称部分的谱方法，都天然对转置不敏感，也就无法恢复路由方向。这一点对 interpretability、训练动态分析、attention head 健康度诊断都有意义。尤其是当研究者试图用单个谱指标解释 hallucination、长上下文失败或 head specialization 时，这篇论文提醒你：如果诊断对象本身抹掉了反对称信息，那么再精细地调统计量也补不回来。","technical_approach":"$26","experiments":"实验重点不是大规模 benchmark 刷分，而是验证理论预测是否在真实模型和数据上成立。文中提到长度控制后，原始 AUROC 最多会下降 0.28，说明不少看似有效的谱特征其实部分依赖长度混淆。这个结果本身就很重要，因为它直接影响任何基于 attention 谱量的诊断结论可信度。\n\n在 failure mode 分析上，作者报告了数据集间的极性差异：HaluEval 更偏 bottleneck routing，MedHallu Pythia。这个观察说明方向信息不是普遍强信号，却在特定结构中不能忽略。\n\n还有一类实验是架构签名验证。uniform causal attention 的 1/5 下界和 window attention 的 O(w/n) 穿透行为，为经验统计提供了参照。作者据此考察不同位置编码/注意力机制下，多少 head 会跌破 1/5，用来区分架构族。虽然这部分更偏分析性而非性能导向，但对理解 attention 结构差异很有帮助。\n\n从基座研究员角度看，实验的局限也很清楚：它没有把这些诊断直接接到训练 loss spike、数据污染、长上下文退化等更贴近训练的问题上，因此当前更像一套机制分析工具箱，而不是可直接部署的训练监控方案。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不建议逐行啃完证明。先看 It asks a more basic diagnostic question: if self-attention is treated as a bipartite transport operator from queries to keys, what can spectral attention diagnostics actually detect, and what do they fundamentally miss? The authors argue that many existing spectral methods only capture transport capacity or mixing, but not directionality. As a result, two mechanistically opposite failure modes—over-concentrated bottlenecks and over-diffuse routing—can look deceptively similar under symmetric spectral summaries. The core contribution is to place a formal structural limit on that indistinguishability.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research is more methodological than directly pretraining-oriented, but it matters for mechanistic analysis and diagnostic design. The paper does not propose a better hallucination detector. Instead, it clarifies the capability boundary of a whole class of attention diagnostics: any method that depends only on singular values or on the symmetric component is inherently blind to orientation. That matters for interpretability, training diagnostics, and analyses of head specialization. If one tries to explain hallucination, long-context failure, or routing collapse with a single spectral statistic, this paper is a useful warning: once antisymmetric information has been projected away, no amount of refinement on the symmetric side can recover direction.","technical_approach":"$27","experiments":"$28","reading_guide":"$29","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇会修正你看 attention 谱图的习惯：凡是只看对称谱的诊断都可能把方向性抹平；读定理和 causal/window 两个解析例子就够了。","en":"This should update how you read attention spectra: any diagnostic built only on the symmetric part can erase directionality; the theorem and the causal/window examples are the parts to read."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Dominik Dahlem","Diego Maniloff","Mac Misiura"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["attention-analysis","spectral-diagnostics","transport","hallucination"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04893","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.04893","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.04971","title":"Why Geometric Continuity Emerges in Deep Neural Networks: Residual Connections and Rotational Symmetry Breaking","abstract":"Weight matrices in deep networks exhibit geometric continuity -- principal singular vectors of adjacent layers point in similar directions. While this property has been widely observed, its origin remains unexplained. geometric continuity。作者关心的不是“它是否存在”，而是“它为什么会稳定出现、依赖哪些结构条件、在不同投影矩阵上为何表现不同”。全文给出的答案很明确：仅靠深层训练并不会自然得到这种连续性；至少需要两类条件共同成立——残差连接提供跨层一致的梯度驱动，打破旋转对称性的模块（激活函数、归一化）把各层约束到共享坐标系里，阻止层间表示在等价旋转轨道上自由漂移。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值主要在机制层，而不是直接给出新训练配方。它把一个经验现象拆成了可验证的训练动力学解释：为什么残差网络里会出现跨层可对齐的结构，为什么某些投影矩阵连续性强、另一些弱，为什么层共享、层裁剪、低秩结构分析在部分模块上更容易成功。对做 LLM 预训练的人，这篇文章提供了一个有用视角：层间结构相似性并不只是“训练后偶然长出来的冗余”，而是由残差梯度耦合和对称性破缺共同塑造的。这个解释有助于理解参数共享、深度压缩、表示几何、训练稳定性之间的关系。贡献量级偏中等，更像一篇把现象讲透的机制论文，而不是会立刻改写训练范式的方法论文。","technical_approach":"技术路线分三层。第一层是定义和测量。作者用相邻层权重矩阵 SVD 后的主右奇异向量 v1、主左奇异向量 u1 的绝对余弦相似度来度量连续性，并补充了一个按奇异值平方加权的全谱版本，用来区分“只有头部方向对齐”还是“多方向共同对齐”。第二层是可控消融。作者在 16 层、宽度 256 的 toy residual MLP 上系统拆解残差、激活、LayerNorm、数据结构等因素。核心结论是：残差连接让各层累计梯度方向更相似，因此各层权重更新会朝相近方向积累；但如果网络保持旋转对称，层与层仍可在等价坐标系中各自漂移，连续性不会稳定保留。激活和归一化的作用就在这里——它们不是简单提供非线性，而是打破线性链路中的旋转规范自由度。作者还专门构造了“非线性但保持旋转对称”的激活，发现它不能产生连续性，这一步把“非线性”与“对称性破缺”清楚分开。第三层是把机制映射回 transformer。作者分析不同投影矩阵与 residual stream 的读写关系：Q、K、Gate、Up 主要从 residual stream 读取，因此在输入空间表现出较强 v1 连续性；O、Down 主要写回 residual stream，因此在输出空间表现出较强 u1 连续性；V 因为邻近位置缺少足够强的非线性约束，连续性显著更弱。文中还指出激活更倾向把连续性集中到主方向上，而 LayerNorm 会把这种对齐扩展到更多奇异方向，这解释了不同模块谱结构的差异。","experiments":"实验分 toy MLP 和小型 transformer 两部分。toy MLP 使用 MNIST，网络为 D=256、L=16 的残差 MLP。作者通过去掉残差、替换激活、移除或替换归一化等方式做因果消融，观察相邻层主奇异向量相似度和有效秩变化。关键发现包括：1）残差是跨层对齐的必要条件，因为它显著提高累计梯度的层间相似性；2）普通激活和 LayerNorm 都能打破旋转对称，但作用不同，前者让连续性集中在主奇异方向，后者让更多谱方向参与；3）一个保持旋转对称的非线性激活不能保留连续性，说明真正起作用的是 symmetry breaking。小型 transformer 在 WikiText-103 上验证 projection-specific continuity。作者报告在预训练的 Llama-3.1-8B 中也能看到同样模式：Q/K/Gate/Up 的输入空间连续性高，O/Down 的输出空间连续性高，V 低。最有信息量的直接消融是去掉 MLP activation 后，Gate 的 v1 continuity 从 0.66 降到 0.37，而困惑度只变化约 3%，说明连续性并不是性能变化的副产品，而是与具体结构模块强相关的几何性质。摘要截断了部分完整表格数值，但从正文给出的这些数字已经足够支撑主要论点。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，但建议把引言、Section 4 的 toy MLP 消融、Section 4.3 关于旋转对称与非线性激活的实验、以及 dominant singular directions, termed geometric continuity. The question is not whether this pattern exists, but why it emerges reliably, what architectural conditions are required, and why it appears strongly in some transformer projections but weakly in others. The full paper argues that continuity is not an automatic byproduct of depth or optimization. It requires two ingredients together: residual connections create cross-layer gradient coherence, and symmetry-breaking components such as activations and normalization anchor layers to a shared coordinate frame, preventing rotation drift along functionally equivalent directions.","importance":"Its value is mainly mechanistic rather than algorithmic. The paper turns an empirical regularity into a training-dynamics explanation: why residual networks develop cross-layer structural alignment, why some projections are more compressible or shareable than others, and why layer pruning or cross-layer parameter sharing often works better in specific submodules. For pretraining researchers, the useful takeaway is that layerwise similarity is not merely accidental redundancy. It is shaped by residual gradient coupling plus symmetry breaking. That perspective is relevant to parameter sharing, depth compression, representation geometry, and training stability. The contribution is moderate in scope but strong in explanatory clarity.","technical_approach":"$2a","experiments":"$2b","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper, but it is worth reading the introduction, the toy-MLP ablation section, the symmetry-breaking experiment with the rotation-preserving nonlinear activation, and the transformer projection analysis. Focus on figures showing adjacent-layer singular-vector similarity across depth, the relation between cumulative gradient directions and weight singular directions, and the collapse of continuity after removing activation or replacing it with a rotation-preserving nonlinearity. In a continual learning setting, such agents can suffer from plasticity loss: their ability to learn new skills from new experiences diminishes over training. Recently, Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) networks have been reported to enable scaling laws and facilitate the learning of diverse skills. However, in continual reinforcement learning settings, their performance can degenerate as learning proceeds, indicating ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-06T10:02:53+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_04712.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究 MoE 策略在持续强化学习中为什么会逐步失去学习新任务的能力，并尝试用可计算的谱指标来约束这种 plasticity loss。","importance":"对 LLM 预训练的直接相关性不强，但对 MoE 长程训练稳定性有参考价值。它把“可塑性下降”转成 eNTK 有效秩下降的问题，提供了一个比只看 reward 或梯度范数更结构化的诊断角度。对长期在线训练、RL pretrain、持续学习中的 MoE 路由退化有启发，尤其是如何监控专家特征是否塌缩到少数更新方向。","technical_approach":"论文先用经验 Neural Tangent Kernel 描述策略在函数空间里的更新方向。若 eNTK 的谱熵有效秩下降，表示模型能利用的函数更新方向变少，新任务到来时更难快速适应。直接构造 eNTK 代价很高，因此作者推导 MoE 的分层块分解，把谱可塑性的代理量写成加权 expert feature matrix 的有效秩问题。SPHERE 的做法是在这个加权特征矩阵上加 Parseval penalty，促使专家特征近似正交、分布在超球面上，减少专家表示共线和功能塌缩。它不同于简单的负载均衡或参数重置：负载均衡主要约束 token 或状态分配是否均匀，SPHERE 约束的是专家特征能否提供多样的函数更新方向。","experiments":"实验覆盖 MetaWorld 和 HumanoidBench 的持续学习设置，并与未正则化 MoE、已有 plasticity 方法进行比较。论文报告 SPHERE 相对普通 MoE 在 MetaWorld 上平均成功率最高提升 133%，在 HumanoidBench 上最高提升 50%。关键现象是：普通 MoE 随训练推进 eNTK 有效秩下降，后续任务性能退化；SPHERE 能在训练后期维持更高的 spectral plasticity，并带来更稳定的跨任务成功率。消融重点应在正则项强度、专家数量、Top-K 路由、DS-MoE 设置，以及谱代理指标和真实性能之间的相关性。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 25–35 分钟。优先看 Introduction 中对 spectral plasticity 的定义，再看方法部分从 eNTK 到 weighted expert feature matrix 的推导，以及 SPHERE 正则项的形式。实验部分重点看 MetaWorld/HumanoidBench 的持续任务曲线、有效秩随训练变化的图、正则强度消融。若关注 LLM MoE，可跳过 MDP 基础定义，重点思考这个指标是否能迁移到 token-level MoE：例如用 expert hidden states 或 FFN activation 构造类似的谱代理，监控长训练中专家功能是否变窄。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies why MoE policies lose the ability to learn later tasks in continual reinforcement learning, and proposes a tractable spectral regularizer to preserve plasticity.","importance":"The work is not directly about LLM pretraining, but it is relevant to long-horizon MoE training. Its useful contribution is a diagnostic view: plasticity loss can be described as a decline in the effective rank of the empirical NTK, meaning that functional updates collapse into fewer directions. For LLM researchers, this is a useful analogy for monitoring expert specialization, expert collapse, and reduced adaptability during online RL, continual pretraining, or long MoE runs.","technical_approach":"The paper formalizes plasticity as diversity in function-space update directions, measured by the spectral-entropy effective rank of the empirical Neural Tangent Kernel. Since forming the full eNTK is too expensive, the authors derive a layerwise block decomposition for MoE policies and obtain a proxy based on a weighted expert feature matrix available from forward passes. SPHERE then applies a Parseval-style penalty to this matrix, encouraging expert features to be less collinear and more evenly spread on a hypersphere. This differs from standard MoE load-balancing losses: load balancing controls routing frequency, while SPHERE controls the geometry of expert features and the diversity of functional update directions.","experiments":"The evaluation uses continual learning benchmarks from MetaWorld and HumanoidBench. SPHERE improves average success over an unregularized MoE baseline by up to 133% on MetaWorld and up to 50% on HumanoidBench. The main diagnostic result is that vanilla MoE policies show a declining eNTK effective rank as training proceeds, while SPHERE maintains higher spectral plasticity and improves later-task performance. The important ablations are the regularization strength, number of experts, Top-K routing choices, DS-MoE variants, and the correlation between the spectral proxy and downstream success.","reading_guide":"Skim time: 25–35 minutes. Focus on the definition of spectral plasticity, the derivation that turns the eNTK objective into a weighted expert-feature proxy, and the exact SPHERE regularizer. In the experiments, prioritize the continual-learning curves, the effective-rank plots, and the regularization ablations. For LLM MoE work, the useful question is whether a similar feature-spectrum diagnostic can be built from expert activations during long pretraining or RL fine-tuning.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"从NTK角度推导MoE的光谱可塑性损失机制，提出的正则化方法可迁移到LLM MoE的持续预训练场景，重点看第3章的代理指标推导","en":"Derives spectral plasticity loss mechanism for MoEs from NTK theory; the proposed regularization is transferable to LLM MoE continual pretraining, focus on Section 3 proxy derivation."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Lirui Luo","Guoxi Zhang","Hongming Xu","Cong Fang","Qing Li"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["mixture-of-experts","spectral plasticity","continual learning","neural tangent kernel","deep reinforcement learning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04712","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.04712","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.04830","title":"Concurrence of Symmetry Breaking and Nonlocality Phase Transitions in Diffusion Models","abstract":"Diffusion models undergo a phase transition in a critical time window during generation dynamics, with two complementary diagnoses of criticality. A time window is critical if interventions there strongly affect final semantics. For nonlocality, they compare global denoising against locally approximated denoising, and use sliding-window switches between local and global denoisers. Signal Propagation in Long-Range Linear Recurrences","abstract":"We study signal propagation in linear recurrent models at finite width. While existing signal propagation theory relies predominantly on the infinite-width limit, it remains unclear for how long that approximation remains accurate when recurrent depth $t$ grows jointly with width $n$. This question is especially relevant for modern recurrent sequence models, whose natural operating regime involves long input sequences, i.e., large $t$. We derive exact finite-width formulas for the hidden state s","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-06T16:44:44+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_05113.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是：在线性递归模型里，经典的无限宽度信号传播理论到底能在多长序列长度上保持准确。已有理论大多假设宽度 n 趋于无穷，此时隐藏态能量会按均值场规律传播；但对现代长序列递归模型，时间步 t 往往和宽度一起变大，有限宽度误差会不断累积。论文聚焦一个更尖锐的问题：对随机初始化的线性 recurrence，有限宽度偏离会在什么 depth-width 联合尺度上从可忽略变成主导，并给出精确而不是粗糙下界的刻画。","importance":"对预训练研究，它不是直接给出新模型或新训练法，而是补上了长程递归建模里一个很基础的稳定性问题。价值主要有三层。第一，它说明 recurrent/SSM/LRU 这类长上下文架构不能简单沿用 feedforward 的“深度到宽度同阶才失效”直觉；在线性 recurrence 中，临界尺度更早，达到 t≈√n 就开始从无限宽近似过渡。第二，它把“同一权重矩阵被反复使用会强化主谱方向”这一直觉做成了可计算的有限宽度公式，这对理解长程训练初期的爆炸/偏置来源很有帮助。第三，它给架构和初始化设计一个明确约束：如果希望随机初始化下的信号传播仍接近 mean-field，序列长度与状态维度不能随意配，至少在线性骨架上要尊重 √n 级别的边界。这对分析 SSM、LRU、复数递归核以及更一般的共享参数长链模型都有启发。","technical_approach":"方法上，论文选了两个最干净的对象：无输入的线性 RNN，以及带输入注入的 linear recurrent unit。隐藏态在每一步都由同一个复高斯随机矩阵 W 作用，矩阵元素独立采样，方差为 1/n。作者关心的量不是单条轨迹，而是归一化后的期望平方范数，也就是 signal energy。核心技术贡献有两部分。\n\n第一部分是精确有限宽度公式。不同于无限宽分析只跟踪均值场递推，这里直接利用复高斯矩阵的谱与矩结构，推导出任意有限 n、任意时间步 t 下隐藏态能量的闭式或准闭式表达。这样做的关键好处是，有限宽度修正项不再只是 big-O 级别，而能看见它如何随 t 和 n 共同增长。\n\n第二部分是 joint scaling regime 的划分。论文给出三个区间：当 t=o(√n) 时，有限宽度修正整体消失，无限宽近似有效；当 t 与 √n 同阶时，进入临界窗口，有限宽度效应不再可忽略，但仍处于可描述的 crossover；当 t≫√n 时，主导谱方向被重复乘法放大，signal energy 明显偏离无限宽预测并出现爆炸趋势。这个结论和 feedforward 网络里常见的临界深度 L~n 很不一样，原因就在于 recurrence 重复使用同一随机矩阵，而不是每层重新采样独立矩阵。\n\n论文还讨论了为什么采用复数权重。一个原因是现代 LRU/SSM 常用复特征值表示振荡动力学；另一个原因是复高斯 ensemble 在解析上更干净，能得到 sharp 结果。作者同时给出实高斯情形偏离无限宽预测的一个充分条件，说明现象并非纯粹依赖复数设定。若从建模角度看，这篇工作的真正创新不在模型，而在把“共享矩阵导致更快积累有限宽度误差”从经验判断推进到精确尺度律。","experiments":"这篇论文的实验更像理论验证，而不是大规模 benchmark。关键结果围绕理论公式与不同 scaling regime 的数值吻合展开。核心观察包括：\n\n1. 在 t=o(√n) 区间，有限宽度下测得的 hidden-state energy 与无限宽预测几乎重合，误差随 n 增大而收缩。\n2. 在 t≈c√n 的临界区间，曲线开始系统性偏离 mean-field 预测，但偏离方式与理论给出的 crossover 公式一致，而不是突然失稳。\n3. 在 t≫√n 时，能量增长明显快于无限宽理论，出现由少数谱方向主导的爆炸行为；这比先前工作只证明“足够大时会爆”更细，因为这里能定位失效发生的尺度。\n4. 对 LRU 与简单线性 RNN，两类模型都表现出相同的临界规律，说明关键因素是共享线性递归核，而不是是否有输入注入。\n5. 论文还比较了复高斯与实高斯设定，表明实值情形也会出现偏离，只是理论 sharpness 主要建立在复数情形上。\n\n从你给出的全文片段里，没有看到完整实验表格中的具体 n、t 网格和误差数值，因此不适合编造精确百分点。但可以确定，实验的重点不是“性能提升多少”，而是“理论曲线是否准确描述有限宽度能量在不同 t/√n 区间的行为”。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不需要逐行推导。建议先看 Introduction，把它和 feedforward 临界深度 L~n 的已知结论对照起来，抓住这篇文章真正回答的问题：为什么 recurrent 的临界尺度提前到 √n。然后重点看给出精确 finite-width signal energy 公式的主定理，以及划分 subcritical / critical / supercritical 三个 regime 的命题或图。若时间有限，优先看展示 t/√n 重标度后曲线如何对齐的图，这通常最能说明结论是否 sharp。\n\n如果想深一点，值得看两处。第一，推导里到底是哪一步把“重复使用同一矩阵”转成了谱方向强化，这关系到结论能否迁移到更一般的共享参数模型。第二，LRU 与无输入线性 RNN 的公式差别，能帮助判断外部输入在多大程度上只改变常数项，而不改变临界尺度。预计阅读时间 30 到 45 分钟；若要跟完整证明，时间会长很多。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise question about linear recurrent models: for how long does infinite-width signal propagation remain accurate when sequence length grows together with hidden width? Existing theory mostly lives in the n→∞ regime, where mean-field recursions describe hidden-state energy cleanly. That is not enough for modern long-context recurrent models, because finite-width errors can accumulate over many recurrent steps. The paper targets the sharp breakdown scale of the infinite-width approximation in randomly initialized linear recurrences, and aims to replace earlier coarse lower bounds with exact finite-width characterizations.","importance":"This is not a new pretraining recipe, but it matters for the foundations of long-range sequence modeling. The main contribution is conceptual and quantitative. First, it shows that recurrent models should not inherit the feedforward intuition that finite-width effects become qualitatively important only when depth is on the order of width. For linear recurrences, the critical scale is much earlier: around t≈√n. Second, it turns the common intuition—reusing the same weight matrix repeatedly amplifies dominant spectral directions—into explicit finite-width formulas. That is useful for understanding instability and bias at initialization in LRUs, SSM-like recurrences, and other shared-kernel sequence models. Third, it gives a concrete design constraint: if one wants initialization-time signal propagation to remain close to mean-field predictions, sequence length and state dimension cannot be chosen independently. Even in the clean linear setting, the safe regime is bounded by a √n law rather than an n law.","technical_approach":"$2c","experiments":"$2d","reading_guide":"$2e","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看定理和 regime 图；它不会直接改 pretrain 配方，但会约束你对长程 recurrent 架构初始化理论的信任范围。","en":"Read the theorems and regime diagram; it will not change a pretraining recipe directly, but it narrows when initialization theory for long recurrent models should be trusted."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Max Planck Institute of Quantum Optics"],"authors":["Mariia Seleznova"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["signal-propagation","finite-width","recurrent-models","theory","long-context"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05113","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.05113","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.05115","title":"Manifold Steering Reveals the Shared Geometry of Neural Network Representation and Behavior","abstract":"Neural representations carry rich geometric structure; but does that structure causally shape behavior? To address this question, we intervene along paths through activation space defined by different geometries, and measure the behavioral trajectories they induce. In particular, we test whether interventions that respect the geometry of activation space will yield behaviors close to those the model exhibits naturally. Concretely, we first fit an activation manifold $M_h$ to representations and ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-06T16:46:03+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_05115.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的核心问题是：神经网络内部表征的几何结构，是否不仅是描述性的现象，而且会因果性地约束模型行为。更具体地说，常见 activation steering 默认激活空间是欧氏空间，用一条直线方向去推模型；作者质疑这个假设，认为如果真实表征落在弯曲流形上，那么线性干预会穿过低密度、非自然的表示区域，导致输出轨迹失真。论文因此把问题改写为：能否用“沿表示流形的路径”来做干预，并验证这种几何是否与行为空间中的自然变化一一对应。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接相关性有限，它更偏机制分析与可控干预，而不是训练目标、数据或优化本身。但它有两个启发点。第一，它提醒人们不要把线性表示假设当成默认真理；如果大模型内部概念组织更接近低维弯曲流形，那么很多基于线性探针、线性 steering、单方向编辑的结论都可能只在局部成立。第二，它把“表示几何”和“行为几何”联系起来，提供了一种更强的因果检验框架：不是只看能不能改输出，而是看干预路径是否沿着模型自然会走的行为轨迹。对理解 ICL 中概念结构、对齐干预、可解释控制有参考价值，但离预训练方法本身还有一层距离。","technical_approach":"方法分三步。第一步，分别拟合 activation manifold 和 behavior manifold。作者选取具有已知结构的任务，例如星期、月份、字母、年龄这类循环或序列概念，以及 in-context 学到的图结构任务；在视频世界模型里还测试了物理位置。对每个任务，他们从模型内部某层激活中提取对应样本点，拟合一个表示流形 Mh；同时把模型输出在任务相关概念上的概率分布视为行为点，拟合行为流形 My。这里的关键不是简单降维可视化，而是定义流形上的 geodesic distance，并比较它与欧氏距离的差异。\n\n第二步，做 geometry-aware steering。基线方法是常见的 linear steering：在激活上加一个方向向量并调节强度。作者提出的 manifold steering 则不是走直线，而是在拟合出的 Mh 上沿测地线或近似测地路径移动激活。这样做的动机是让干预始终停留在自然激活分布附近，避免切穿流形外部区域。\n\n第三步，验证表示几何与行为几何的对应关系。论文报告两类结果：一类是距离层面的 scaled isometry，即 Mh 上的测地距离与 My 上的测地距离高度对齐，而两者都与欧氏距离不一致；另一类是路径层面的因果验证，即若在激活空间沿 Mh 干预，输出分布会沿 My 的自然轨迹变化。作者还反过来做优化：直接要求行为轨迹贴着 My 走，再看恢复出的激活路径是否自动贴着 Mh 的曲率。这个双向验证比单纯展示“某种 steering 更好用”更扎实。\n\n从方法创新看，真正新的是把 steering 从“找一个方向”改成“选一个几何”，并用 geodesic 语言统一线性 steering、流形 steering 和行为约束 steering。它不是提出新的预训练算法，而是给 activation intervention 提供了更合理的几何框架。","experiments":"实验覆盖 LLM 和视频世界模型两类系统。语言侧任务包括循环概念如 weekdays、months，顺序概念如 ages、letters，以及 in-context 学到的多维图结构。核心结果有几类。\n\n1. 距离对齐：在这些任务上，activation manifold 与 behavior manifold 的 geodesic distance 呈现接近等距的对应关系，通常只差一个缩放因子；而如果直接用欧氏距离，这种对应明显变差。\n2. 路径自然性：线性 steering 往往让行为轨迹穿过低密度区域，出现不自然中间分布、流畅性下降或多样性塌缩；manifold steering 生成的行为轨迹更贴近模型未干预时的自然输出变化。\n3. 反向恢复：若直接在行为空间优化一条沿 My 的路径，得到的最优激活轨迹会自动弯向 Mh，而不是保持直线，这支持“表示几何约束行为几何”而非仅仅相关。\n4. 跨模态一致性：类似现象不仅出现在语言概念任务，也出现在视频世界模型中的物理位置控制，说明这不是某个单一文本任务的偶然现象。\n\n从给出的全文片段看，作者强调的是几何关系和干预轨迹质量，而不是传统 benchmark 分数。没有完整表格数字时，比较稳妥的阅读方式是关注图中距离相关系数、轨迹可视化和 ablation，而不是追求单一指标。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合机制分析方向的读者泛读。先看 Introduction 和方法总览，抓住一句话：作者不是在找更好的 steering vector，而是在问 activation space 应该配什么几何。然后优先看 weekday/month 这类最直观的案例图，因为循环概念最容易看出“直线切过去”和“沿流形绕过去”的差别。接着看距离对齐结果，确认 activation manifold 与 behavior manifold 的 geodesic 是否真的近似等距；这是整篇文章最关键的证据。\n\n如果只花 20 到 30 分钟，建议看三部分：任务设定、manifold steering 方法、线性与流形干预的对比图。若想深一点，再看反向优化行为路径那节，因为它提供了更强的因果支持。对做预训练的人，值得思考的不是是否立刻采用这套方法，而是：很多我们习惯用线性工具分析的表示，是否其实只在局部近似线性。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether the geometry of neural representations is merely descriptive or whether it causally shapes model behavior. Standard activation steering assumes Euclidean geometry: pick a direction in activation space and move along a straight line. The authors argue that this assumption is often wrong. If natural activations lie on a curved manifold, then linear steering cuts through low-density, off-manifold regions and induces unnatural behavioral transitions. The central question becomes: can interventions that follow the geometry of the representation manifold produce behavioral trajectories that match the model’s own natural behavior manifold?","importance":"Its relevance to pretraining is indirect. This is primarily a mechanistic interpretability and intervention paper, not a training-method paper. Still, it offers two useful lessons for foundation-model research. First, it challenges the default linear representation assumption. If concepts are organized on curved low-dimensional manifolds rather than along isolated linear directions, then many conclusions drawn from linear probes, linear steering, and single-direction editing may only hold locally. Second, it proposes a stronger causal test for representation analysis: not just whether an intervention changes outputs, but whether the induced path in behavior space follows the model’s natural geometry. That is useful for understanding controllability, concept structure in in-context learning, and the limits of linear tools.","technical_approach":"$2f","experiments":"$30","reading_guide":"$31","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"看干预 setup 和线性 steering 对照即可；结论若能在 LLM residual stream 上复现，会影响 activation editing 的默认几何假设。","en":"Read the intervention setup and the linear-steering control; if it transfers to LLM residual streams, it changes the default geometry assumption behind activation editing."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Stanford University","University College","Northeastern University","Harvard University","aStanford University","dHarvard University","bUniversity College","cNortheastern University"],"authors":["Daniel Wurgaft","Can Rager","Matthew Kowal","Vasudev Shyam","Sheridan Feucht","Usha Bhalla","Tal Haklay","Eric Bigelow","Raphael Sarfati","Thomas McGrath","Owen Lewis","Jack Merullo","Noah Goodman","Thomas Fel","Atticus Geiger","Ekdeep Singh Lubana"],"notable_authors":["Thomas McGrath"],"tags":["interpretability","activation-steering","representation-geometry","manifold-learning","causal-intervention"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05115","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.05115","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.05176","title":"Understanding In-Context Learning for Nonlinear Regression with Transformers: Attention as Featurizer","abstract":"Pre-trained transformers are able to learn from examples provided as part of the prompt without any weight updates, a remarkable ability known as in-context learning (ICL). paired-execution trace corpus，用配对执行轨迹来测量 evaluator-channel blocking 下的排名不稳定。关键设计是固定任务、系统 checkpoint 和最终评测器，只改变评测器信号是否能进入 agent 的 selector。若屏蔽 selector 输入边界处的 evaluator-derived signal 后，系统排名位移明显下降，就说明排名变化部分来自 evaluator-to-selector 耦合，而不是普通评测噪声。该资源注册了 576,000 个 cells，其中 96,000 个实际执行。筛查架构是模块化的，包含四类实现：学习式 influence proxy、无需训练模型的规则式 channel-exposure ratio、counterfactual sensitivity proxy，以及 sparse human-audit proxy。作者也明确限制了适用范围：目标是操作性筛查，不声称恢复完整潜在因果机制；分析要求 selector 输入边界的 hook-completeness 达到至少 0.80。","experiments":"在一个包含 60 个 agent-repair 系统的池子上，只改变 evaluator 配置就会造成明显排名变化：两个排名的 Kendall tau 为 0.74，中位系统排名位移为 3.1，top-three 身份有 7 个系统发生变化。其中 4 个 top-three swaps 可追踪到 evaluator-selector 耦合通道，例如 selector 读取 judge rationale tokens、把 verdict scalar 拼入 prompt、reranker logit 传给 planner、延迟记忆中回放 evaluator score。对这些通道做 blinding 后，耦合系统的排名位移下降 3.2 到 4.5 个位置；非耦合系统的下降低于 1.5。验证方面，作者使用 80 个 source-level channel-surgery cases 做机制锚定验证，并在 79 个独立发现的 cases 上得到 pooled AUROC 0.83。AuditRepairBench-Lite 保持与完整排行榜 Kendall tau 0.88，同时去掉训练模型依赖，运行成本低于 24 GPU-hours。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 Introduction、Corpus definition and operational target，以及实验中关于 blinding intervention 和 产出的偏好数据、轨迹数据或自动标签时，需要检查评测器输出是否污染了生成过程。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a specific source of instability in agent-repair leaderboards: some repair agents use evaluator-derived signals during internal candidate selection, so their ranking changes when the evaluator configuration changes.","importance":"This is not a foundation-model pretraining paper, but it is relevant to agent evaluation and code-agent benchmarking. The main lesson is that evaluation quality depends not only on the final scoring rule, but also on whether evaluator outputs leak into the agent’s selector. If judge rationales, confidence scores, or reranking logits influence internal repair selection, the leaderboard starts measuring adaptation to the evaluator channel as well as repair capability. This matters when such benchmarks are used to select models, generate preference data, or build training traces for coding agents.","technical_approach":"AuditRepairBench is a paired-execution trace corpus designed to measure ranking instability under evaluator-channel blocking. The key intervention keeps the task instances, agent checkpoints, and final evaluator fixed, while blocking evaluator-derived information at the selector input boundary. If rank displacement decreases substantially after this blinding operation, the displacement is attributed to evaluator-to-selector coupling rather than ordinary evaluation noise. The corpus contains 576,000 registered cells, with 96,000 executed cells. The screening pipeline is modular and includes four implementations: a learned influence proxy, a rule-based channel-exposure ratio that uses no trained model, a counterfactual sensitivity proxy, and a sparse human-audit proxy. The authors also keep the claim scoped: the benchmark provides an operational screen, not a full causal certificate, and it assumes sufficient hook completeness at the selector boundary.","experiments":"On a pool of 60 agent-repair systems, changing only the evaluator configuration leads to a Kendall tau of 0.74 between rankings, a median per-system rank displacement of 3.1, and seven top-three identity changes. Four of those seven swaps are traced to evaluator-selector coupling channels, including selector access to judge rationale tokens, verdict scalar concatenation in prompts, reranker-logit passthrough to the planner, and delayed-memory replay of evaluator scores. After blinding the relevant selector inputs, coupled systems show rank-displacement reductions of 3.2 to 4.5 positions, while non-coupled systems show reductions below 1.5. The validation uses 80 source-level channel-surgery cases and an independent-discovery protocol with 79 cases, reaching pooled AUROC 0.83. AuditRepairBench-Lite preserves the leaderboard with Kendall tau 0.88, removes trained-model dependencies, and runs in under 24 GPU-hours.","reading_guide":"Skim the Introduction, the corpus definition, and the sections describing the blinding intervention and top-three swap analysis. The useful part is not the absolute ranking of individual agents, but the measurement design that separates ordinary leaderboard variance from evaluator-channel coupling. Estimated reading time is 20 to 30 minutes. For foundation-model training work, the practical takeaway is to audit whether evaluator outputs contaminate agent trajectories before using benchmark traces, labels, or rankings as training signal.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"看 corpus 定义和 ranking flip 指标；它提醒 repair agent 评测里 evaluator 泄漏比模型差距更能改排名。","en":"Read the corpus definition and ranking-flip metrics; it is a useful warning that evaluator leakage can move repair-agent rankings more than model quality."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Shanghai Jiao Tong University","Shanghai Maritime University"],"authors":["Yuelin Hu","Zhenbo Yu","Zhengxue Cheng","Wei Liu","Li Song"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["coding-agent","agent-repair","evaluation-leakage","leaderboard-stability"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04624","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.04624","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.05112","title":"Rollout Pass-Rate Control: Steering Binary-Reward RL Toward Its Most Informative Regime","abstract":"SWE-bench-style agentic reinforcement learning relies on expensive stateful trajectories, yet substantial compute is wasted on sampled rollout groups with skewed pass rates, where binary rewards provide a weak contrastive signal. We frame this inefficiency as a pass-rate control problem and show that a 50% pass rate is the most informative operating point: it maximizes reward entropy, the probability of surviving group filtering, RLOO advantage energy under GRPO, and success--failure contrastive","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-06T16:44:38+00:00","category_id":"coding_agent","cover_url":"/covers/2605_05112.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 binary-reward RLVR，尤其是 SWE-bench 这类 stateful agent 环境里的一个效率问题：rollout 很贵，但很多 rollout group 的通过率极端偏斜，导致组内几乎没有成功/失败对比信号，GRPO/RLOO 这类相对优势更新拿不到足够信息，算力被浪费在“低信息密度”的轨迹上。作者把这个问题形式化为 rollout pass-rate control，并主张最有信息量的工作点是 50% 通过率。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接贡献，更接近 post-train / RL 训练效率工作，但有两个启发点值得记住。第一，它把 binary reward 下“什么样的 rollout 最有训练价值”说清楚了，不再只是经验上过滤 all-zero / all-one group，而是把 pass rate 与 reward entropy、GRPO advantage energy、对比结构联系起来。第二，它提出的 Prefix Sampling 不是靠外部 teacher 或 oracle hint，而是重用当前策略自己产生的轨迹前缀，在固定 rollout 成本下把训练样本推向更有信息的区域。这种“控制训练信号分布而不是单纯多采样”的思路，对 RL post-train、agentic continual training，甚至某些 curriculum 设计都有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法分成两部分：目标分析和具体控制器。\n\n先是目标分析。作者从四个角度论证 50% pass rate 是 binary-reward RL 的高信息区：reward entropy 在 p=0.5 最大；GRPO/RLOO 下的 advantage energy 在成功与失败数量平衡时最大；组过滤后能保留下来的概率在中间区域更高；成功-失败对比对的数量也在 k=N/2 时最多。这里的关键不是一个新损失，而是把“为什么 1/8 或 7/8 这种组虽然没被过滤掉，但仍然训练价值不高”讲清楚。\n\n在此基础上，作者提出 Prefix Sampling。设每组有 N=8 个 rollout，先按 pass count k 分桶：k∈{0,8} 的退化组直接丢弃；k∈{3,4,5} 视为已足够平衡，直接训练；k∈{1,2} 视为 too-hard，保存其中一个成功轨迹前缀，后续重放给新 rollout 作为 head start；k∈{6,7} 视为 too-easy，保存一个失败轨迹前缀，后续重放作为 handicap。这样做的目的，是把后续 continuation 的成功率往 50% 拉，而不是让整条轨迹更容易或更难。\n\n具体实现上，Prefix Sampling 在 stateful agent 环境里重建前缀状态，然后只对当前策略生成的 continuation 计算损失，重放前缀 token 被 mask 掉，不参与优化。这一点很重要，因为否则方法会退化成对旧轨迹的行为克隆。作者还设计了 Adaptive Prefix controller，为不同 pass-rate bucket 学一个前缀比例 r_b∈[0.05,0.95]，决定重放到轨迹长度 T 的哪个边界 M=floor(r_b*T)。对 too-hard bucket，较长成功前缀能提高后续成功概率；对 too-easy bucket，较长失败前缀能增加后续失败概率，从而把组内分布拉回中间区域。方法是双向的，这比只救难题的 hinting 更完整，因为过于容易的组同样缺少对比信号。\n\n和已有方法相比，区别主要有三点：一是不依赖外部强模型、参考解或人工提示；二是目标不是提升单条轨迹成功率，而是控制组内通过率分布；三是适用于 stateful、多轮、长轨迹 agent 环境，而不是只在静态题目上做 sample filtering。","experiments":"实验重点是训练效率，而不是最终上限性能。\n\n1. SWE-bench-style agentic RL：作者报告在 Qwen3-14B 和 Qwen3-32B 上，Prefix Sampling 能在不牺牲最终 verified score 的前提下，更快达到基线最佳点。具体数字是：达到 baseline 最佳 verified score 所需 benchmark evaluation steps 最多减少 1.76×；端到端训练速度分别提升 2.01×（Qwen3-14B）和 1.55×（Qwen3-32B）。这说明收益主要来自更高的信息密度，而不是更大的训练预算。\n\n2. AIME 2025 reasoning：摘要和引言提到方法也迁移到数学推理场景，说明它并不依赖软件工程环境的特殊结构。不过从全文定位看，主战场仍是 binary-reward、group-based RLVR，而不是通用 reasoning RL。\n\n3. 机制验证：作者强调收益来自 rerollout pass rate 被推向 50% 附近，而不是简单的数据增广。也就是说，关键中介变量是 pass-rate distribution 的重塑。若这一点在正文图表中呈现清楚，那么它比单纯报 wall-clock speedup 更有说服力。\n\n4. 消融方向：从方法描述可以推断，值得关注的消融包括：只对 too-hard 组做前缀重放、只对 too-easy 组做重放、固定前缀长度 vs 自适应前缀比例、是否 mask replayed prefix token、不同 bucket 边界设置。论文声称有 mechanistic and ablation evidence，阅读时应重点确认这些消融是否真的支持“50% 是最优控制目标”而不是某个实现细节偶然有效。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓住一句话：这不是在发明新的 RL 算法，而是在控制 binary-reward rollout 的信息密度。\n\n建议阅读顺序：\n1. 先看 Introduction 里对 group filtering、GRPO/RLOO 和 50% pass-rate 目标的动机；\n2. 再看 Method 中关于四个信息量指标为何都在 50% 附近最优；\n3. 然后看 Prefix Sampling 的流程图和 bucket 设计，理解 head start / handicap 的双向控制；\n4. 最后看实验里 pass-rate 分布变化、达到同等 verified score 的步数、以及关键消融。\n\n如果只打算花 20–30 分钟，优先看方法图、主结果表、以及证明 50% 目标合理性的那一节。真正值得确认的是：收益是否来自更好的 credit signal，而不是隐藏的额外 compute 或 replay 偏置。\n\n对做 pretrain 的读者，阅读重点不在具体 benchmark，而在训练信号工程：当 reward 很稀疏且 rollout 很贵时，控制样本落在“最有梯度信息”的区域，往往比继续堆采样更有效。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets an efficiency bottleneck in binary-reward RLVR, especially in stateful agent settings such as SWE-bench-style environments. Rollouts are expensive, but many rollout groups have highly skewed pass rates, so they contain little success-failure contrast. Under GRPO/RLOO-style updates, those groups provide weak learning signal even if they are not fully degenerate. The paper formulates this as a rollout pass-rate control problem and argues that the most informative operating regime is around a 50% pass rate.","importance":"This is primarily a post-training RL efficiency paper rather than a pretraining paper, but it has a useful lesson for foundation-model training: not all sampled trajectories are equally informative, and the right objective is often to shape the distribution of training signal rather than simply collect more data. The paper makes the notion of “informative binary-reward rollouts” more precise by linking pass rate to reward entropy, GRPO advantage energy, and contrastive structure.
